Under high behavior-deployment mismatch, policy-consistent risk lowers selected-decision ECE from 0.108 to 0.053; DCTC lowers it from 0.039 to 0.022 at matched acceptance; PRCC removes false-feasible admissions on an 18-action catalog ($0.197\rightarrow0.000$); and enabling all three components reduces trajectory failure from 0.181 to 0.083 in a factorial ablation.

</details>


### [28] [Harness-Aware Distillation for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02858)
*Moonseok Choi,Taehong Moon,Giung Nam,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向带harness的小语言模型agent蒸馏，直接研究harness信息、长程反馈与动作偏好，可迁移到coding agent。

TL;DR: 提出 Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD)，在固定 harness 下聚焦教师超出 harness 的增量能力；通过动作偏好对比与有效性检查补充同策略蒸馏，无需任务奖励、成功标签或未来信息；在多个长时程智能体基准上优于同策略蒸馏基线，并减少无效循环、提升错误恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准蒸馏让模型模仿教师完整输出，并将 harness 当作输入，未能针对性学习 harness 无法提供、但学生实际需要的教师特有信息，例如正确利用 harness 信息行动。

Method: HAD 将蒸馏目标限制为教师模型超出 harness 的贡献，并在同策略蒸馏上加入：1）动作偏好，对比同一教师在有/无 harness 信息下的动作，评分发生在学生自身推理之后；2）有效性检查，过滤偏好动作与 harness 记录冲突的偏好对。方法不依赖任务奖励、成功标签或未来信息。

Result: 在多个长时程智能体基准和模型上，HAD 在相同固定 harness 下超过同策略蒸馏基线；分析显示 HAD 更少陷入无效循环、更常从错误中恢复，并倾向于将可学习反馈自适应地保留在权重中，同时从 harness 读取状态信息。

Conclusion: 在 harness 固定不变的智能体蒸馏场景中，应聚焦教师超出 harness 的增量能力；HAD 以无需额外奖励、标签或未来信息的方式实现这一点，提升长时程智能体表现与鲁棒性。

中文摘要：语言模型智能体在部署时附带一个 harness（运行框架），即模型周围用于管理其上下文、工具和反馈的软件。当此类智能体被蒸馏到更小的模型时，harness 保持不变，因此学生模型主要需要教师模型特有、且 harness 无法提供的能力，例如根据 harness 信息正确行动。然而，标准蒸馏模仿教师模型的完整输出，并将 harness 视为输入的一部分。我们提出 Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD)，它将蒸馏重点放在教师模型超出 harness 所额外提供的内容上。HAD 通过两个组件补充 on-policy distillation（同策略蒸馏）：一个动作偏好，对比同一教师模型在有和没有 harness 信息时的动作，并在学生模型自身推理之后进行评分；以及一个有效性检查，丢弃其偏好动作与 harness 记录相矛盾的偏好对。我们表明，这种对比为学生模型提供了仅模仿教师模型无法提供的信息，并且 HAD 不需要任务奖励、成功标签或未来信息。在多个长时程智能体基准和模型上，HAD 在相同固定 harness 下优于 on-policy distillation 基线。我们的分析表明，与基线相比，HAD 进入无效循环的次数更少，从错误中恢复的次数更多，并表明它自适应地将可学习的反馈保留在权重中，同时从 harness 读取状态信息。

Original abstract: Language model agents are deployed with a harness, the software around the model that manages its context, tools, and feedback. When such an agent is distilled into a smaller one, the harness stays in place, so the student mainly needs the teacher-specific abilities that the harness cannot provide, such as acting correctly on harness information. Standard distillation, however, imitates the teacher's full outputs and treats the harness as part of the input. We propose Harness-Aware Distillation (HAD), which focuses distillation on what the teacher adds beyond the harness. HAD complements on-policy distillation with two components: an action preference that contrasts the same teacher's actions with and without the harness information, scored after the student's own reasoning, and a validity check that drops preference pairs whose preferred action contradicts the harness records. We show that the contrast gives the student information that imitating the teacher alone cannot provide, and HAD needs no task rewards, success labels, or future information. Across multiple long-horizon agent benchmarks and models, HAD outperforms on-policy distillation baselines with the same fixed harness. Our analysis shows that HAD enters fewer unproductive loops and recovers from errors more often than the baselines, and suggests that it adaptively keeps learnable feedback in its weights while reading state information from the harness.

</details>


### [29] [PsyEvo: A Personalized Counseling Agent That Self-Evolves at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02885)
*Yuting Yan,Shihao Xu,Junhao Yu,Mingcong Zuo,Lu Chen,Nan Xiang,Haiyang Geng,Dongjie Tao,Minghao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出测试时自演化的个性化咨询agent，其技能后验、偏好优化与信用分配机制对agent自改进有参考价值。

TL;DR: PsyEvo 是一个基于 LLM 的心理咨询框架，通过 HBSP、LiPO、SOCA 实现测试时个性化与回应策略改进；模拟评估中 PsychEval 总体得分 7.684，消融显示各组件均有条件贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康需求大但专业咨询师短缺，LLM 咨询师可扩展；但仅离线训练难以在测试时适配个体并持续从互动中学习。

Method: 提出 PsyEvo，包含 HBSP 维护每位来访者技能后验以个性化干预选择；LiPO 用跨来访者偏好更新共享回应适配器以改进技能表达；SOCA 通过一致性比较与序数投影提供偏好和轨迹信用。

Result: 在共享在线队列适应的模拟来访者评估中，PsychEval 总体得分 7.684，三次匹配运行均优于各组件变体；移除组件使平均总分下降 0.138–0.171。

Conclusion: 完整框架中各组件的条件性贡献得到支持，PsyEvo 展现了测试时个性化与策略改进的可扩展心理咨询潜力；代码已开源。

中文摘要：心理健康障碍影响着全球人口的很大比例，然而训练有素的从业者的持续短缺使大多数人无法获得充分照护。基于大语言模型（LLM）的咨询师为提供可扩展的对话式心理支持提供了一个有前景的方向。仅靠离线模型训练，适应个体来访者或在测试时从持续的治疗互动中学习留下的空间有限。我们提出 PsyEvo，一个基于 LLM 的咨询框架，通过三个组件在测试时同时实现针对特定来访者的个性化和回应策略改进：分层贝叶斯技能策略（HBSP）通过维护一个从会话反馈更新的每位来访者技能后验，个性化应应用何种干预；会话间列表式偏好优化（LiPO）通过从跨来访者偏好证据更新共享回应适配器，改进所选技能的表达方式；状态条件序数信用分配（SOCA）通过一致性检查的比较和序数投影，为前两个组件提供候选偏好和轨迹信用。在具有共享在线队列适应的模拟来访者评估中，PsyEvo 在 PsychEval 上获得 7.684 总体得分，并在三次匹配运行中的每一次都超过所有组件变体。在共享配置下，移除单个组件会使平均总体得分降低 0.138–0.171，支持完整脚手架内各组件的条件性贡献。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/Lingxi-mental-health/PsyEvo 获取。

Original abstract: Mental health disorders affect a substantial proportion of the global population, yet a persistent shortage of trained practitioners leaves the majority without adequate care. Large language model (LLM)-based counselors present a promising direction for delivering scalable conversational psychological support. Offline model training alone leaves limited room to adapt to individual clients or to learn from ongoing therapeutic interaction at test time. We introduce PsyEvo, an LLM-based counseling framework that enables both client-specific personalization and response-policy improvement at test time through three components: Hierarchical Bayesian Skill Policy (HBSP) personalizes what intervention to apply by maintaining a per-client skill posterior updated from session feedback; Inter-session Listwise Preference Optimization (LiPO) improves how the selected skill is expressed by updating a shared response adapter from cross-client preference evidence; and State-conditioned Ordinal Credit Assignment (SOCA) supplies candidate preferences and trajectory credit to the two components through consistency-checked comparisons and ordinal projection. In simulated-client evaluation with shared online cohort adaptation, PsyEvo obtains 7.684 Overall on PsychEval and exceeds every component variant in each of three matched runs. Removing individual components lowers mean overall score by 0.138--0.171 under the shared configuration, supporting conditional contributions within the complete scaffold. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lingxi-mental-health/PsyEvo

</details>


### [30] [Interpreting at Write Time: A Policy Ablation for Multi-Goal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02897)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted to PALM workshop at NeurIPS 2026

Selection reason: 针对多目标agent记忆的写入策略消融，直接研究长期记忆摘要应服务哪些目标，可启发agent记忆设计。

TL;DR: 长期助手记忆的摘要写入策略应针对具体目标；逐目标摘要优于无目标或全目标摘要，写入时解释仅惠及后续提问的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期助手必须压缩历史，但摘要在写入时选择保留什么，且该选择发生在知道哪个目标会查询之前；多个常设目标对哪些内容值得保留存在分歧，因此需要研究记忆应为什么目标而总结。

Method: 提出并比较三种摘要写入策略：无目标总结、单一全目标摘要、逐目标摘要并联合读取；在多个模型和事件流上评估，固定读取步骤，仅改变写入方式。

Result: 目标确实分化：不同目标摘要彼此重叠低于摘要与自身重写的重叠；逐目标摘要在相关性、完整性、准确性上最佳；全目标摘要甚至不如低预算的中性摘要；写入时解释仅对之后提问的目标有收益。

Conclusion: 记忆摘要不应追求目标中立的单一份摘要，而应按目标分别写入并联合读取；在写入阶段解释目标有价值，但收益受限于后续实际查询的目标。

中文摘要：一个长期运行的助手无法保留它见过的所有内容，因此它会进行总结。总结并非中立：保留什么内容是根据某种关于记录用途的观念来选择的，而这种选择在任何人知道用户的哪一个常设目标会来查询之前就已一次性做出。目标很少对发生了什么产生分歧。它们分歧在于其中哪些部分值得占用空间。一旦历史长到无法重读，摘要就会取代原始流，而它遗漏的内容也就消失了。我们研究当记忆同时服务于多个常设目标时，它应该为什么而总结。三种策略给出了不同答案：在没有目标视角的情况下总结；写一个覆盖每个目标的摘要；或为每个目标写一个摘要并将它们一起读取。我们在多个模型和事件流上比较它们，固定读取步骤，以便只有写入不同。目标确实会分化：为不同目标写出的摘要彼此之间的重叠，少于一个摘要与其自身重写之间的重叠。逐目标摘要在相关性、完整性和准确性上胜出，而全目标摘要甚至输给以预算一小部分写成的中性摘要。在写入时进行解释是有回报的，但只对之后提出的那个目标有回报。

Original abstract: A long-running assistant cannot keep everything it has seen, so it summarises. Summarising is not neutral: what is kept is chosen against some notion of what the record is for, and that choice is made once, before anyone knows which of the user's standing goals will ask. Goals rarely disagree about what happened. They disagree about which parts of it were worth the space. Once the history is too long to re-read, the summary replaces the stream, and whatever it left out is gone. We ask what a memory should summarise for when it serves several standing goals at once. Three policies answer differently: summarise with no goal in view, write one summary covering every goal, or write one summary per goal and read them together. We compare them across several models and event streams, holding the read step fixed so that only the write differs. The goals do pull apart: summaries written for different goals overlap each other less than a summary overlaps a rewrite of itself. Per-goal summaries win on relevance, completeness and accuracy, and the all-goal summary loses even to the neutral one written at a fraction of its budget. Interpreting at write pays off, but only for the goal that later asks.

</details>


### [31] [HASTE: Evolving Agent Harnesses Against Emerging Attacks Using Sparse Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02920)
*Xiqiao Xiong,Moxin Li,Zhixin Ma,Ouxiang Li,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多agent框架从稀疏威胁证据演化agent harness，直接涉及harness自动更新、安全约束与攻击反馈。

TL;DR: HASTE 通过多智能体对抗式生成安全规范与攻击案例，从稀疏威胁证据中自动演化 Agent harness，在降低攻击成功率的同时保持良性任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent harness 通过安全约束防御不安全动作，但人工适配难以跟上快速涌现的攻击，而用于自动演化的信号又常常稀疏，仅有威胁报告中的简短描述或少量示例。

Method: 提出 HASTE 多智能体框架，让安全规范生成与攻击案例生成对抗交互，并将评估结果反馈回两者，从稀疏威胁证据中迭代演化 Agent harness。

Result: 在多种骨干模型、攻击类型和证据形式上，HASTE 一致降低攻击成功率，并保持良性任务效用；代码已开源。

Conclusion: HASTE 能基于稀疏威胁证据实现 harness 演化，防御超出初始证据的新兴攻击。

中文摘要：Agent harness 在防御中发挥着关键作用，通过执行安全约束来防止不安全动作。然而，快速涌现的攻击使人工 harness 适配相形见绌，从而推动了自动化 harness 演化。不过，可用于 harness 演化的信号往往稀疏，例如威胁报告和预印本中的简要描述或少量攻击示例。为解决这一局限，我们提出了 HASTE，一个多智能体框架，它通过安全规范生成与攻击案例生成之间的对抗性交互，从稀疏威胁证据中演化 Agent harness。安全规范引导 harness 更新以解决已识别的安全漏洞，而攻击案例则在每次更新后探查剩余的安全漏洞。通过将评估结果反馈回这两个过程，HASTE 能够针对超出最初观察证据的新兴攻击实现 harness 演化。在多种骨干模型、攻击类型和证据形式上的实验结果表明，HASTE 在保持良性任务效用的同时，持续降低攻击成功率。代码可在 https://github.com/xxiqiao/HASTE 获取。

Original abstract: Agent harnesses play a critical role in defenses by enforcing safety constraints to prevent unsafe actions. However, rapidly emerging attacks outpace manual harness adaptation, motivating automated harness evolution. Yet the signals available for harness evolution are often sparse, such as brief descriptions or a few attack examples in threat reports and preprints. To address this limitation, we introduce HASTE, a multi-agent framework that evolves agent harnesses from sparse threat evidence through an adversarial interplay between safety-specification generation and attack-case generation. Safety specifications guide harness updates toward addressing identified safety vulnerabilities, while attack cases probe for remaining safety vulnerabilities after each update. By feeding evaluation outcomes back into both processes, HASTE enables harness evolution against emerging attacks beyond the initially observed evidence. Experimental results across multiple backbone models, attack types, and evidence forms show that HASTE consistently reduces attack success rates while preserving benign-task utility. The code is available at https://github.com/xxiqiao/HASTE.

</details>


### [32] [Positive-Unlabeled Learning for Agent Safety False Alarm Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02925)
*Xichen Yan,Chongyang Gao,Kezhen Chen,Guangyi Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 19 pages

Selection reason: 面向agent安全监控的假警报审计，提出PU排序框架，可迁移到agent可靠性审计与告警筛选。

TL;DR: 该工作将语言模型智能体安全监控中的误报审核形式化为正-未标注排序问题，提出两阶段框架，在无需警报安全标签且不修改底层监控器的情况下，显著提升误报识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全监控器虽能保障语言模型智能体与外部工具和环境交互，但保守监控会产生大量误报，占用审核资源并降低对警报的信任。由于误报与真实警报在原始监控器分数中混杂，确定可靠截断值仍需大量人工验证。实践中仅有少量已验证安全的未警报轨迹可用，而警报未标注，因此误报审核天然适合建模为正-未标注（PU）排序问题。关键难点是监控器导致的选择偏差：观察到的安全参考是被监控器接受的正例，而真正要恢复的隐藏安全警报恰是监控器误报的样本。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段使用 Trust-aware PU Supervision，将安全参考适配到警报域，并保护可能的误报免受过度负向压力；同时使用 Reliability-gated Rank Distillation 将多个 PU 参考模型的一致排序偏好蒸馏到单个学生模型。第二阶段使用 Consensus-guided Structural Refinement，结合分层安全参考支持、警报关系以及预测的参考共识来改进学生排序。该框架拟合时不需要警报安全标签，且不改变底层监控器。

Result: 在主流安全监控器上，该方法取得 0.6444 的宏 AUPRC，较八个评估的 PU 基线高出 5.27--16.98 个绝对百分点；与评估中最强的 PU 基线 PULDA 相比，在 5% 审核预算下多恢复 33.3% 的误报。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在缺乏警报安全标签的情况下进行误报审核排序，并保持底层监控器不变；实验表明其优于多个 PU 基线，可在有限审核预算下更有效地发现误报。

中文摘要：安全监控器有助于保护与外部工具和环境交互的语言模型智能体，但保守监控可能产生大量误报，消耗大量审核资源并削弱对警报的信任。由于误报和真实警报在原始监控器分数中往往相互交织，获得可靠阈值仍需要大量人工验证。在实践中，可能有一小部分经过验证安全的未警报轨迹可用，而警报仍未被标注，这自然将误报审核转化为正-未标注（positive-unlabeled, PU）排序问题。关键挑战是监控器引起的选择偏差，因为观察到的安全参考被监控器接受，而感兴趣的隐藏安全警报恰恰是它错误标记的那些，这使得观察到的正例难以代表待恢复的正例。为应对这一挑战，我们提出一个两阶段框架，其中 Trust-aware PU Supervision 将安全参考适配到警报域，并保护可能的误报免受过度负向压力，而 Reliability-gated Rank Distillation 将多个 PU 参考模型的一致排序偏好整合到一个学生模型中。随后，Consensus-guided Structural Refinement 使用分层安全参考支持、警报关系和预测的参考共识来改进学生排序。该框架在拟合时不需要警报安全标签，并保持底层监控器不变。在主流安全监控器上，我们的方法实现了 0.6444 的宏 AUPRC，比八个评估的 PU 基线高出 5.27--16.98 个绝对百分点；与评估中最强的 PU 基线 PULDA 相比，在 5% 审核预算下多恢复 33.3% 的误报。

Original abstract: Safety monitors help safeguard language-model agents interacting with external tools and environments, but conservative monitoring can generate many false alarms, consuming extensive review resources and weakening trust in alerts. Because false and genuine alarms often remain interleaved in native monitor scores, obtaining a reliable cutoff still requires substantial manual verification. In practice, a small set of verified-safe non-alarmed trajectories may be available while alarms remain unlabeled, naturally casting false-alarm auditing as a positive-unlabeled (PU) ranking problem. The key challenge is monitor-induced selection, since observed safe references are accepted by the monitor, while the hidden safe alarms of interest are precisely those it incorrectly flags, making the observed positives poorly representative of the positives to be recovered. To address this challenge, we propose a two-stage framework in which Trust-aware PU Supervision adapts safe references toward the alarm domain and protects plausible false alarms from excessive negative pressure, while Reliability-gated Rank Distillation consolidates consistent ordering preferences from multiple PU reference models into a single student. Consensus-guided Structural Refinement then improves the student ranking using hierarchical safe-reference support, alarm relations, and predicted reference consensus. The framework requires no alarm safety labels for fitting and leaves the underlying monitor unchanged. Across mainstream safety monitors, our method achieves a macro AUPRC of $0.6444$, outperforming eight evaluated PU baselines by 5.27--16.98 absolute percentage points; compared with PULDA, the strongest evaluated PU baseline, it recovers 33.3% more false alarms at a 5% review budget.

</details>


### [33] [When to Compile a Computer-Use Agent? Measuring Payback and Making Compilation Decisions for Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02932)
*Yulong Ming,Jie Xu,Zihan Wu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 27 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: 研究computer-use agent何时把重复GUI流程编译为程序以节省token，直接关联agent代码化、复用与成本优化。

TL;DR: PACE 通过测量编译成本与复用节省，并在线决策何时将 GUI 流程编译为程序，在理论保证下平均降低 token 成本 17.3%-24.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将智能体重复执行的 GUI 流程编译成程序可节省 token，但编译成本不确定且未来复用未知，导致是否以及何时编译难以决策。

Method: 提出 PACE，包含测量协议与在线编译算法：记录成功和失败编译成本，比较智能体与程序在匹配任务输入上的执行成本以估计每次节省与回本计数；基于历史任务到达和编译结果，将预估未来节省与含失败成本的编译成本比较，并用累积预算约束执行和编译动作。

Result: 在所述动作成本假设下，每次任务到达后的总成本至多为全程使用智能体成本的 1+ε 倍；成功编译的估计回本计数（不含源智能体运行）为 2-16 次使用；在记录任务到达的模拟中，ε=0.25 时 PACE 平均比 ReAct 降低 token 成本 17.3%，比 AutoRPA 改编降低 24.9%，比 ToolPro 改编降低 17.3%。

Conclusion: PACE 能在编译成本与未来复用不确定的情况下进行回报感知的在线编译决策，并在模拟中显著降低 token 成本。

中文摘要：将智能体重复执行的 GUI 过程编译为程序可以降低其 token 成本。然而，衡量回报并决定何时编译存在两个挑战。第一，编译成本不确定，因为尝试可能需要修复，并且仍可能无法生成可用程序。第二，未来复用情况未知，因为任务可能停止到达，或者 GUI 漂移可能使程序停止工作。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 PACE（Payback-Aware Compilation from Experience，基于经验回报感知的编译），一个具有测量协议和在线编译算法的系统。测量协议记录成功和失败的编译成本，并在匹配的任务输入上比较智能体与程序的执行成本，以估计每次使用的节省和回本计数。利用这些测量，在线算法根据过去的任务到达和编译结果，将估计的未来节省与编译成本（包括失败尝试）进行比较。在允许任一动作之前，它根据观察到的任务到达所确定的累积预算检查执行和编译费用。在所述动作成本假设下，每次到达后的总成本至多为用智能体运行每个任务成本的 $1+ε$ 倍。对于成功的编译尝试，排除源智能体运行后的估计回本计数为 2-16 次使用。在使用记录的任务到达进行的模拟中，PACE 平均将 token 成本相比 ReAct 降低 17.3%，相比 AutoRPA 改编降低 24.9%，相比 ToolPro 改编降低 17.3%（$ε=0.25$）。

Original abstract: Compiling GUI procedures that agents execute repeatedly into programs can reduce their token costs. However, measuring payback and deciding when to compile have two challenges. First, compilation costs are uncertain because attempts can require repair and still fail to produce a usable program. Second, future reuse is unknown because tasks may stop arriving or GUI drift may stop the program from working. To address these challenges, we propose PACE (Payback-Aware Compilation from Experience), a system with a measurement protocol and an online compilation algorithm. The measurement protocol records successful and failed compilation costs, and compares agent and program execution costs on matched task inputs to estimate per-use savings and payback counts. Using these measurements, the online algorithm compares estimated future savings with compilation costs, including failed attempts, based on past task arrivals and compilation outcomes. It checks execution and compilation charges against a cumulative budget determined by observed task arrivals before allowing either action. Under stated action-cost assumptions, total cost after each arrival is at most $1+ε$ times the cost of running every task with the agent. For successful compilation attempts, estimated payback counts excluding source agent runs are 2-16 uses. In simulations using recorded task arrivals, PACE reduces token costs by 17.3% compared with ReAct, 24.9% with the AutoRPA adaptation, and 17.3% with the ToolPro adaptation on average ($ε=0.25$).

</details>


### [34] [Continual Graph Memory for Mathematical Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02945)
*Junyi Zhang,Jinxi Yu,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Jiachen Lu,Zhi Zhang,Xinjie He,Hyunsik Chae,Ethan Ji,Alexander K Taylor,Vigyan Sahai,Yiwen Kou,Kai-Wei Chang,Raghu Meka,Nanyun Peng,Amit Sahai,Terence Tao,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 为数学研究agent提出持续图记忆与依赖检索/经验复用，对长程agent记忆和过程组织很有参考价值。

TL;DR: Ansatz 通过持续图记忆组织长时程证明搜索并复用跨问题探索知识，在多项数学任务和开放问题上取得突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学前沿问题常需大量智能体长时间并行构造证明，产生海量中间结果，如何组织这些结果并复用先前探索知识是核心挑战。

Method: 提出 Ansatz 数学研究智能体，核心为持续图记忆：统一图记忆表示事实、计划、反例及其关系；依赖感知检索；证据敏感策展器；范围化召回。

Result: 在全部十个 First Proof Second Batch 任务上实现闭合，并完成四项组件研究；自主解决贾米森毛毛虫猜想和埃尔德什问题 289、348、488，并在若干开放问题上取得部分进展。

Conclusion: Ansatz 能维持和恢复长时程数学搜索，并展现出解决开放数学研究问题的强大能力。

中文摘要：利用前沿智能体框架来解决数学研究问题，已成为推动数学发展的一种有效手段。然而，解决数学前沿问题可能需要大量智能体长时间并行工作以构造证明，从而产生海量中间证明结果。在长时程证明搜索过程中组织这些中间结果，并复用先前探索所获得的知识，仍是重大挑战。我们提出 Ansatz，一个围绕持续图记忆（Continual Graph Memory）构建的数学研究智能体；持续图记忆是一种基于图、可演化、跨问题的数学研究记忆系统，它显式地组织整个证明搜索过程，并复用先前问题探索轨迹中的信息。具体而言，我们开发了一个统一的图记忆，用于表示所有中间探索结果，包括事实、计划和反例，并通过边显式表示它们之间的关系；依赖感知检索提供精确目标的局部上下文；证据敏感的策展器（curator）更新研究前沿，并从先前尝试中提炼经验教训；范围化召回则呈现早先的陈述和否定性发现，用于局部重新证明，而非不加批判地复用。实验涵盖了全部十个 First Proof Second Batch 问题的运行，以及四项组件研究。Ansatz 报告在所有十项研究任务上实现闭合（closure），展示了其维持和恢复长时程数学搜索的能力。除这些问题之外，Ansatz 还在无人干预的情况下给出了贾米森毛毛虫猜想（Jamison caterpillar conjecture）以及埃尔德什问题 289、348 和 488 的解答，并在若干开放问题上取得部分进展，表明其解决开放数学研究问题的强大能力。

Original abstract: Using frontier agent harnesses to tackle mathematical research problems has emerged as an effective means of advancing mathematics. However, solving frontier problems in mathematics may require a massive number of agents working in parallel for extended periods to construct proofs, thereby generating an enormous volume of intermediate proof results. Organizing these intermediate results throughout a long-horizon proof-search process and reusing knowledge gained from prior explorations remain major challenges. We present Ansatz, a mathematical research agent built around Continual Graph Memory, a graph-based, evolvable, cross-problem mathematical research memory system that explicitly organizes the entire proof search process and reuses information from exploration trajectories of previous problems. Specifically, we develop a unified graph memory that represents all intermediate exploration results, including facts, plans, and counterexamples, together with edges that explicitly represent the relationships among them; dependency-aware retrieval supplies precisely targeted local context; an evidence-sensitive curator updates the research frontier and distills lessons from prior attempts; and scoped recall surfaces earlier statements and negative findings for local re-proving rather than uncritical reuse. Experiments cover runs across all ten First Proof Second Batch problems, together with four component studies. Ansatz reports closure on all ten research tasks, demonstrating its ability to sustain and resume long-horizon mathematical search. Beyond these problems, Ansatz also produces solutions to the Jamison caterpillar conjecture and Erdős Problems 289, 348, and 488 without human intervention, and makes partial progress on several open problems, illustrating its strong ability to solve open mathematical research problems.

</details>


### [35] [Reasoning with Evidence, Not Merely Rationales: Verifiable Preference Proofs for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02968)
*Yu Hou,Nathaniel Kang,Pengkai Wang,Hua Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出可验证偏好证明与证据/影响力校验，虽用于推荐，但其验证式推理机制可启发agent证据验证。

TL;DR: 提出 PROVE-REC 可验证偏好推理框架：Pass A 生成偏好证明，Pass B 仅凭证明与证据预测，并通过掩蔽证据与移除主张分别验证依据性与影响力，性能提升最高 7.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 生成的偏好推理依据可能与实际用于推荐的信息脱节，出现两类失败：偏好主张缺乏所选证据支撑（依据不足），或对最终排序无实质影响（影响力不足），即依据-影响鸿沟。

Method: 两阶段框架：Pass A 将完整预目标历史压缩为偏好证明（正向/回避主张及关联证据条目）；Pass B 仅用该证明与证据预测下一物品，阻断绕过推理路径。以掩蔽所选证据对比掩蔽对照条目验证依据性，以移除偏好主张后目标物品排序边际的降幅验证影响力，并加入排序保持目标保留完整历史信息。

Result: 在广泛真实世界数据集上，PROVE-REC 持续优于强序列、生成式与 LLM 增强基线，提升最高达 7.45%；受控消融验证了两遍架构与验证目标的有效性；生成的偏好主张依据性更强、对推荐更具影响力，同时保持排序质量。

Conclusion: PROVE-REC 为 LLM 推荐提供了可验证的偏好推理路径，兼顾依据充分性与推荐影响力，并保持排序质量。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）能够从交互历史和评论中推断用户偏好，但它们生成的推理依据可能并不反映实际用于推荐的信息。一个偏好主张可能仅被其所选证据弱支持，或者对最终排序几乎没有影响。我们将这两类失败称为依据-影响鸿沟。我们提出 PROVE-REC，一个用于基于 LLM 的推荐中可验证偏好推理的通用框架。Pass A 将完整的预目标历史转换为紧凑的偏好证明，该证明由与所选证据条目相关联的正向主张和回避主张构成。Pass B 仅使用该证明及其所选证据来预测下一个物品，从而防止推荐器绕过该推理路径。为验证证据到证明的依据性，我们比较掩蔽所选证据与掩蔽一个可比对照条目所产生的效果。为验证证明到推荐的影响力，我们移除一个偏好主张并测量目标物品排序边际的相应下降幅度。一个排序保持目标进一步保留完整历史中的有用信息。在广泛的真实世界数据集上进行的综合实验表明，PROVE-REC 持续优于强序列、生成式以及 LLM 增强的基线，提升幅度最高达 7.45%。受控消融实验证实了两遍架构与验证目标的有效性。此外，PROVE-REC 生成的偏好主张更强地依据于历史证据、对推荐更具影响力，同时保持排序质量。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can infer user preferences from interaction histories and reviews, yet the rationales they generate may not reflect the information actually used for recommendation. A preference claim may be weakly supported by its selected evidence, or may have little effect on the final ranking. We refer to these two failures as the grounding-influence gap. We introduce PROVE-REC, a general framework for verifiable preference reasoning in LLM-based recommendation. Pass A converts the complete pre-target history into a compact preference proof consisting of positive and avoidance claims linked to selected evidence entries. Pass B predicts the next item using only the proof and its selected evidence, preventing the recommender from bypassing the reasoning path. To verify evidence-to-proof grounding, we compare the effect of masking selected evidence with masking a comparable control entry. To verify proof-to-recommendation influence, we remove a preference claim and measure the resulting decrease in the target item's ranking margin. A ranking-preservation objective further retains useful information from the complete history. Comprehensive experiments on wide-ranging real-world datasets demonstrate that PROVE-REC consistently outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with improvements of up to 7.45%. Controlled ablations confirm the effectiveness of the two-pass architecture and verification objectives. Moreover, PROVE-REC produces claims that are more strongly grounded in historical evidence and more influential to recommendation while preserving ranking quality.

</details>


### [36] [Reliable Self-Evolution with Imperfect Proxy Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02975)
*Kangjun Noh,Soyu Kim,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Preprint

Selection reason: 用保形区间和代理奖励校准提升自演化搜索可靠性，可迁移到agent自我改进中的不可靠奖励问题。

TL;DR: 提出CISE：用条件共形推断与逐迭代在线密度比估计构建候选奖励区间，以保守区间奖励指导自演化，并仅在区间完全落入可行域时返回候选；在材料科学任务中实现高保真评估下全为真阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真评估每个候选的成本过高，导致自演化系统依赖低成本但不完美的代理奖励，可能给不可行候选高分；这些假阳性会污染最终输出和后续生成反馈，因此需要统计校准的奖励区间。

Method: CISE利用条件共形推断和逐迭代在线密度比估计构建候选特定奖励区间；使用保守的区间奖励进行演化反馈；仅当所有所需性质区间完全位于各自可行区域内时才返回候选；并在显式独立性与协变量偏移假设下推导固定迭代覆盖结果。

Result: 在三个材料科学自演化搜索任务中，CISE返回的所有候选在高保真评估下均为真阳性；基线方法返回更多候选，但包含假阳性。

Conclusion: 当下游验证预算有限时，更小且更精确的候选短名单具有重要价值；代码仓库为 https://github.com/MLAI-Yonsei/CISE.git。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型（LLM）的自演化搜索是科学发现中一种有前景的方法。然而，在某些领域，对每个候选进行高保真评估的成本高得难以承受。因此，这类场景下的自演化系统依赖低成本但不完美的代理奖励，而这些奖励可能给不可行的候选分配高分。这些假阳性可能污染最终输出以及用于指导后续生成的反馈。这促使人们采用统计校准的奖励区间，以实现更可靠的自演化搜索。我们提出共形区间驱动的自演化（CISE），它利用条件共形推断和逐迭代的在线密度比估计来构建候选特定的奖励区间。CISE将保守的基于区间的奖励用于演化反馈，并且仅当所有所需性质区间完全位于各自可行区域内时才返回候选。我们在显式的独立性与协变量偏移假设下推导了固定迭代覆盖结果。我们在材料科学中的三个自演化搜索任务上评估了CISE。在我们的实验中，CISE返回的所有候选在高保真评估下均为真阳性，而基线方法返回更多候选，但包含假阳性。这些结果凸显了当下游验证预算有限时，更小、更精确的候选短名单的价值。我们的代码仓库见 https://github.com/MLAI-Yonsei/CISE.git。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based self-evolving search is a promising approach to scientific discovery. However, high-fidelity evaluation of every candidate is prohibitively expensive in some domains. Self-evolving systems in such settings therefore rely on low-cost but imperfect proxy rewards, which may assign high scores to infeasible candidates. These false positives may contaminate both the final output and the feedback used to guide subsequent generations. This motivates statistically calibrated reward intervals for more reliable self-evolving search. We propose Conformal Interval-Driven Self-Evolution (CISE), which constructs candidate-specific reward intervals using conditional conformal inference and iteration-wise online density-ratio estimation. CISE uses conservative interval-based rewards for evolutionary feedback and returns candidates only when all required property intervals lie entirely within their respective feasible regions. We derive fixed-iteration coverage results under explicit assumptions of independence and covariate shift. We evaluate CISE on three self-evolving search tasks in materials science. In our experiments, all candidates returned by CISE are true positives under high-fidelity evaluation, whereas the baselines return more candidates but include false positives. These results highlight the value of a smaller, more precise shortlist when downstream validation budgets are limited. Our repository is available at https://github.com/MLAI-Yonsei/CISE.git.

</details>


### [37] [PLCWorld: Benchmarking LLM-Generated PLC Programs in Closed-Loop Plant Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02982)
*Yunji Kim,Yunseok Lee,Hyunwoo Seo,Jaerim Choi,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 36 pages, 9 figures. Project website: https://yunji0516.github.io/PLCWorld/

Selection reason: 提出LLM生成PLC程序的闭环仿真基准，含执行反馈、安全约束与生成-验证工作流，直接关联coding agent评测。

TL;DR: PLCWorld是一个将ST执行与仿真设备响应和传感器反馈耦合的闭环执行环境与基准，用于评估LLM生成PLC程序的任务成功与安全违规；包含100个任务、473个任务-条件对，并显示GPT-5.5在困难任务上性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM生成的PLC程序是否满足任务要求与安全约束，不能只分析代码，必须观察控制命令对设备与工件状态的实际影响；当前缺乏统一的闭环执行环境和基准。

Method: 提出PLCWorld：耦合ST执行、仿真被控对象响应与传感器反馈；基于工业PLC程序和工程文档中的控制关系，构建Motion Control与Material Handling的100个合成任务、473个任务-条件对，难度按控制依赖范围划分；采用统一协议分别报告Task Success与Safety Violation；验证包括从业者评审、参考与替代程序、定向反例、规范-评估器对齐检查、与独立ST运行时比较；并提出Execution Gap关联提交配置文件的接受与后续任务失败或安全违规。

Result: 参考与替代程序满足适用案例；542个定向反例在至少一个注册条件下激活指定评估器规则；直接GPT-5.5在Easy案例任务成功率为82.70%，Hard案例仅25.10%；对六个LLM和四个生成-验证工作流的评估进一步暴露完成度、安全性与生成成本差异。

Conclusion: PLCWorld提供了公开可用的闭环执行环境、基准任务与基线实现，支持对LLM生成PLC程序进行任务成功、安全违规及执行差距的系统评估。代码、仿真环境、基准任务和基线实现见https://yunji0516.github.io/PLCWorld/。

中文摘要：可编程逻辑控制器（PLC）通过读取传感器输入并发出控制命令来协调工业设备。评估大语言模型（LLM）生成的PLC程序是否满足任务要求与安全约束，需要观察其命令如何影响设备与工件状态。我们提出PLCWorld，一个通用的闭环执行环境与基准，将结构化文本（ST）执行与仿真的被控对象响应和传感器反馈耦合起来。基于从工业PLC程序和工程文档中识别出的控制关系，PLCWorld包含跨运动控制和物料搬运的100个合成任务与473个已注册的任务-条件对，难度由控制依赖范围定义。一套通用协议分别报告任务成功与安全违规。验证结合了从业者评审、参考程序与替代程序、定向反例、规范-评估器对齐检查，以及与独立ST运行时的比较。参考程序和替代程序满足其适用案例，而全部542个定向反例在至少一个已注册条件下激活其指定的评估器规则。执行差距将提交配置文件的接受与后续任务失败或观察到的安全违规联系起来。在所构建的任务组中，直接使用GPT-5.5在简单案例上达到82.70%的任务成功率，但在困难案例上仅为25.10%。对六个LLM和四个改编的生成-验证工作流的评估进一步揭示了完成度、安全性与生成成本之间的差异。我们的代码、仿真环境、基准任务和基线实现公开于https://yunji0516.github.io/PLCWorld/。

Original abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) coordinate industrial equipment by reading sensor inputs and issuing control commands. Evaluating whether large language model (LLM)-generated PLC programs satisfy task requirements and safety constraints requires observing how their commands affect device and workpiece states. We introduce PLCWorld, a common closed-loop execution environment and benchmark that couples Structured Text (ST) execution with simulated plant responses and sensor feedback. Grounded in control relations identified in industrial PLC programs and engineering documentation, PLCWorld contains 100 synthetic tasks and 473 registered task-condition pairs across Motion Control and Material Handling, with difficulty defined by control-dependency scope. A common protocol reports Task Success and Safety Violation separately. Validation combines practitioner review, reference and alternative programs, targeted counterexamples, specification-evaluator alignment checks, and comparisons with independent ST runtimes. Reference and alternative programs satisfy their applicable cases, while all 542 targeted counterexamples activate their designated evaluator rules under at least one registered condition. Execution Gap relates submission-profile acceptance to subsequent task failure or observed Safety Violation. Across the constructed task groups, direct GPT-5.5 achieves 82.70% Task Success on Easy cases but 25.10% on Hard cases. Evaluations of six LLMs and four adapted generation-and-verification workflows further expose differences between completion, safety, and generation cost. Our code, simulation environment, benchmark tasks, and baseline implementations are publicly available at https://yunji0516.github.io/PLCWorld/.

</details>


### [38] [DyadMem: A Long-Term Memory Benchmark of How Agents Work with Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03020)
*Yifei Tao,Xinyu Zhong,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Fanyi Wang,Tengda Guo,Wentao Qiu,Ying Wang,Liujian Tang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出长期agent记忆基准DyadMem，联合评估用户事实与关系型agent记忆，并提供全流程评测。

TL;DR: 提出DyadMem与用户条件关系型智能体记忆（URAM），构建双领域、全流程记忆基准，联合标注用户侧记忆与关系型智能体记忆，并用Gold-Memory/Full-Pipeline两种QA设置评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注用户事实、偏好或可跨用户复用经验，忽略关系特定的智能体记忆；同时仅用最终答案QA评估长历史交互，导致评估不完整、不可靠。

Method: 提出URAM新定义与DyadMem基准，沿同一多会话轨迹联合标注用户侧记忆和URAM，形成6类记忆；包含3,065个episode、50,961个会话、61,210个QA实例，提供会话级Capture/Update黄金标注、查询级Recall支持及Gold-Memory、Full-Pipeline两种QA设置。

Result: 在16个开放权重和4个专有模型上，Gold-Memory QA持续较强，但Full-Pipeline QA急剧下降；定量结果揭示低Capture召回、不完整Recall和不安全删除问题；验证实验显示URAM对全部20个模型均有积极效果。

Conclusion: DyadMem是双领域、全流程记忆基准，通过大量标注推动关系型智能体记忆与长交互评估研究发展。

中文摘要：长期智能体不仅必须记住关于用户的真实信息，还必须记住随着共享历史演变，特定智能体应如何与该用户协作。现有基准主要监督用户事实与偏好，或可跨用户复用的经验，使这种关系特定的智能体记忆保持隐含。此外，多数先前工作仅通过长交互历史上的最终答案QA来评估模型，使评估仍不完整且不可靠。为此，我们提出DyadMem，并提出新定义：用户条件的关系型智能体记忆（User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory, URAM）。DyadMem沿相同多会话轨迹联合标注用户侧记忆与URAM，形成6个记忆类别。总之，它包含3,065个episode、50,961个会话和61,210个QA实例，具有广泛的会话级Capture与Update黄金标注、查询级Recall支持，以及两种QA设置：Gold-Memory和Full-Pipeline。在16个开放权重模型和4个专有模型上，Gold-Memory QA持续表现强劲，但Full-Pipeline QA急剧下降。这种差距明确支持我们的细粒度评估设计。此外，若干定量结果进一步揭示，即使前沿LLM也存在低Capture召回、不完整Recall以及不安全删除问题。我们进一步进行严格实验以验证URAM的有效性，并观察到对所有20个模型均有积极效果。总之，DyadMem是一个双领域、全流程的记忆基准，具有大量标注工作，以推动该领域发展。

Original abstract: Long-term agents must remember not only what is true about a user, but also how a particular agent should work with that user as their shared history evolves. Existing benchmarks primarily supervise user facts and preferences or experience reusable across users, leaving this relationship-specific agent memory implicit. Additionally, most prior works measure the model solely with final-answer QA over long interaction histories, making the assessment still incomplete and unreliable. To this end, we introduce DyadMem with the proposed new definition User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory (URAM). DyadMem jointly annotates user-side memory and URAM along the same multi-session trajectories, resulting in 6 memory categories. To summarize, it includes 3,065 episodes, 50,961 sessions, and 61,210 QA instances, with extensive session-level Capture and Update gold annotations, query-level Recall support, and two QA settings: Gold-Memory and Full-Pipeline. Across 16 open-weight and 4 proprietary models, Gold-Memory QA is consistently strong, yet Full-Pipeline QA drops sharply. Such a gap explicitly supports our fine-grained evaluation design. Additionally, several quantitative results further reveal low Capture recall, incomplete Recall, and unsafe-deletion issues arising from even the frontier LLMs. We further conduct a rigorous experiment to validate the effectiveness of our URAM and observe the positive effects for all 20 models. In summary, DyadMem is a dual-domain, full-pipeline memory benchmark with extensive annotation efforts for advancing the domain's development.

</details>


### [39] [When Numbers Start Talking: Numerical Signalling and Strategic Behaviour Among LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03033)
*Alessio Buscemi,Daniele Proverbio,Alessandro Di Stefano,The Anh Han,German Castignani,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究多智能体 LLM 之间不同消息类型对策略博弈与协作均衡的影响，属于多 agent 通信与可靠性实证，可迁移到多 agent 协作设计。

TL;DR: 研究不同消息类型对LLM智能体策略博弈合作的影响，发现结构化消息无规律地改变收益，数值消息偏离随机且难以解释，监测应优先消息级指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究不清楚不同消息类型如何影响策略博弈结果，以及人格设定是否在其中起作用。

Method: 使用四种流行LLM构建AI智能体，在四个具有不同合作均衡的博弈中，测试自然语言、数值信号、随机序列等消息类型及人格设定的影响。

Result: 结构化消息在多数博弈和LLM中改变最终收益，但无可预测模式；数值消息偏离随机，尤其在明确要求交流时最强且一致，但其符号分布多与收益结构相关并随重复更集中，人类整体难以解释。

Conclusion: AI智能体未必能收敛到稳定均衡；针对受限渠道协调的监测应优先采用可跨模型泛化的消息级指纹，而非行为决策。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体日益在以策略互动为特征的多智能体系统（MAS）中运行。然而，对于不同类型的信息是否以及在多大程度上影响策略博弈的结果，人们知之甚少。通过研究基于四种流行LLM的AI智能体，参与四个具有不同合作均衡的博弈，我们考察不同类型的信息（自然语言、数值信号或随机序列）是否会显著改变每个博弈中的合作水平，并且这种影响是否还取决于所赋予智能体的性格。我们观察到，结构化信息改变了大多数博弈和LLM的最终收益，但没有可预测的模式；这挑战了无论附加能力如何，AI智能体都能收敛到稳定均衡的假设。此外，我们观察到，智能体生成的数值信息偏离随机性，在明确指示智能体进行交流时最为强烈且一致；然而，它们带来了额外的可解释性挑战，因为其符号分布大多与收益结构相关，并通常随重复而变得更集中，但总体上难以被人类解释。因此，针对通过受限渠道进行协调的AI智能体进行监测，应优先考虑消息层面的指纹，这些指纹能够跨模型泛化，而非行为决策，后者则不能。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents increasingly operate in multi-agent systems (MAS) characterised by strategic interaction. However, little is known about whether, and to what extent, different types of messages affect the outcomes of strategic games. By investigating AI agents based on four popular LLMs, playing four games with different cooperation equilibria, we study whether messages of different kinds (natural language, numerical signals, or random sequences) significantly modify the levels of cooperation in each game, also depending on the agents' assigned personalities. We observe that structured messages alter the final payoffs for most games and LLMs, but without a predictable pattern; this challenges the assumption that AI agents can converge to stable equilibria regardless of additional capabilities. Moreover, we observe that agent-generated numerical messages depart from randomness, most strongly and consistently when agents are explicitly instructed to communicate; however, they introduce an additional interpretability challenge, as their symbol distributions are mostly associated with the payoff structure and typically become more concentrated with repetition, but are overall difficult for humans to interpret. Monitoring for coordination of AI agents through restricted channels should thus prioritise message-level fingerprints, which generalise across models, over behavioural decisions, which do not.

</details>


### [40] [hacktrace: behavior-supervised detection of reward hacking during code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03055)
*Hao Jiang,Xin Li,Annan Wang,Yichi Zhang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向代码生成 agent 的 reward hacking 检测，利用生成内部状态监控作弊行为并用于 RL 惩罚，直接相关。

TL;DR: HACKTRACE 复用编码智能体生成代码时的内部状态，在无需额外 token 或前向传递的情况下检测奖励黑客行为，并以 8 毫秒开销达到 0.997 的平均逐问题 AUC，还能在 GRPO 训练中大幅降低作弊通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可能通过删除暴露缺陷的测试等捷径获得通过，而非真正修复代码；检测这类奖励黑客行为需要识别包括失败尝试在内的捷径，但成功与否独立监督是关键。

Method: 发布来自 Qwen3-8B 的 173,561 条带标注多轮编码轨迹；提出 HACKTRACE，一种行为监督式监控器，读取智能体生成代码时已计算的内部状态，并与最终文件的静态特征结合。

Result: HACKTRACE 在 8 毫秒监控开销下取得 0.997 的平均逐问题 AUC，优于再次运行模型进行诚实性问答的监控器；在强 GRPO 惩罚下，将通过解决方案中的作弊比例从 82-91% 降至 1-5%，同时保留诚实正确解并维持高检测准确率。

Conclusion: 监督目标和监控证据来源对于把准确检测转化为有用的训练信号都很重要；生成状态可同时用于高效监控和强化学习中的低成本信号。

中文摘要：一个编码智能体可以通过修复其代码来获得及格分数，也可以通过删除暴露该缺陷的测试来获得及格分数。检测此类奖励黑客行为需要识别尝试性的捷径，包括那些失败的捷径。我们发布了来自 Qwen3-8B 的 173,561 条带标注的多轮编码轨迹，并表明独立于漏洞利用是否成功来监督捷径行为可显著改进检测。我们提出 HACKTRACE，一种行为监督式监控器，它读取智能体在生成代码时已经计算的内部状态。复用这些状态使得在一轮完成之前即可进行监控，而无需额外的语言模型 token 或前向传递。将此证据与最终文件的静态特征结合，可在 8 毫秒监控开销下达到每个问题平均 AUC 0.997，在准确性和延迟方面均优于让模型再次回答一个诚实性问题的监控器。相同的生成状态也为强化学习提供了一种低成本的监控信号。在强 GRPO 惩罚下，HACKTRACE 将通过解决方案中的作弊比例从 82-91% 降至 1-5%，同时保留诚实、正确的解决方案，并在策略演化过程中保持高检测准确率。我们的结果表明，监督目标与监控证据的来源对于将准确检测转化为有用的训练信号都至关重要。

Original abstract: A coding agent can earn a passing grade by fixing its code, or by deleting the test that exposes the bug. Detecting such reward hacking requires recognizing attempted shortcuts, including those that fail. We release 173,561 annotated multi-turn coding trajectories from Qwen3-8B and show that supervising shortcut behavior independently of exploit success substantially improves detection. We introduce HACKTRACE, a behavior-supervised monitor that reads the internal states the agent already computes while generating code. Reusing these states enables monitoring before a turn is complete, without additional language-model tokens or passes. Combining this evidence with static features of the final files achieves a mean per-problem AUC of 0.997 with 8 ms of monitoring overhead, improving both accuracy and latency over monitors that run the model again on an honesty question and answer. The same generation states also provide an inexpensive monitoring signal for reinforcement learning. With strong GRPO penalties, HACKTRACE reduces the cheating share of passing solutions from 82-91% to 1-5%, while retaining honest, correct solutions and maintaining high detection accuracy as the policy evolves. Our results show that both the supervision target and the source of monitoring evidence matter for turning accurate detection into a useful training signal.

</details>


### [41] [MOF-VERIFY: A Failure-Aware Agentic Harness for MOF Hypothesis Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03056)
*Donghyun Lee,Taehoon Lee,Geonhee Ahn,Jieun Kim,Jihyun Park,Suyeon Cho,Yoona Kim,Chaerim Shin,Hoi Ri Moon,Jonggeol Na,Sukho Hong,Jihwan Oh,Soo Kyung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshops XAI4Science and AI4Mat

Selection reason: 提出 failure-aware agentic harness，先诊断结构、文献、证据与计算瓶颈再验证假设，其失败驱动流程可迁移到 agent 验证。

TL;DR: 该工作提出诊断基准与MOF-Verify失败感知智能体框架，用于评估并改进LLM在MOF假设验证中的可靠性，覆盖结构、合成条件、证据充分性和MLIP计算验证，并在多个主干LLM上显著优于直接推理和检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被用于AI驱动的材料协同科学家中的推理，但其验证流程的可靠性仍不清晰；MOF存在标识符多样、合成条件依赖强、证据来源异构以及部分假设需计算等问题，因而构成严苛测试场景。

Method: 构建包含四个任务族的诊断基准：结构锚定、合成条件验证、证据充分性验证和基于MLIP的计算验证；T-MOF-1-3在闭卷、启用检索和预言机证据设置下评估，以定位知识访问、证据获取和推理失败，T-MOF-4单独评估计算验证；据此开发失败感知智能体框架MOF-Verify，在最终判定前处理结构、文献、证据充分性与计算瓶颈。

Result: 在多个主干LLM上，MOF-Verify在假设验证性能上显著优于直接推理和基于检索的基线；基准数据集已发布在https://github.com/IMMS-Ewha/MOF-Verify-Benchmark。

Conclusion: 该诊断基准能够定位知识访问、证据获取和推理中的失败，MOF-Verify可有效提升MOF假设验证可靠性，并通过公开基准促进后续研究。

中文摘要：大语言模型正越来越多地被用作AI驱动的材料协同科学家（Co-Scientists）中的推理组件，但由此产生的验证流程的可靠性仍不明确。金属有机框架（MOFs）提供了一个尤其具有挑战性的场景，因为结构可能以不同标识符出现，合成结果强烈依赖于实验条件，证据分散在异构来源中，并且一些假设需要计算而不仅仅是文献。我们引入一个诊断基准，包含四个任务族，涵盖结构锚定、合成条件验证、证据充分性验证和基于MLIP的计算验证。T-MOF-1-3在闭卷、启用检索和预言机证据设置下进行评估，以定位知识访问、证据获取和推理中的失败，而T-MOF-4则单独评估计算验证。在这些诊断出的失败模式指导下，我们开发了MOF-Verify，一种失败感知的智能体化执行框架，该框架在给出最终判定之前，针对结构、文献、证据充分性和计算瓶颈进行处理。在多个主干LLM上，MOF-Verify在假设验证性能上显著优于直接推理和基于检索的基线。基准数据集发布于https://github.com/IMMS-Ewha/MOF-Verify-Benchmark。

Original abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as reasoning components in AI-driven materials Co-Scientists, yet the reliability of the resulting verification pipeline remains unclear. Metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) provide a particularly challenging setting because structures may appear under different identifiers, synthesis outcomes depend strongly on experimental conditions, evidence is distributed across heterogeneous sources, and some hypotheses require computation rather than literature alone. We introduce a diagnostic benchmark with four task families covering structural grounding, synthesis-condition verification, evidence-sufficiency verification, and MLIP-based computational verification. T-MOF-1-3 are evaluated under closed-book, retrieval-enabled, and oracle-evidence settings to localize failures in knowledge access, evidence acquisition, and reasoning, while T-MOF-4 separately evaluates computational verification. Guided by these diagnosed failure modes, we develop MOF-Verify, a failure-aware agentic harness that targets structural, literature, evidence-sufficiency, and computational bottlenecks before producing a final verdict. Across multiple backbone LLMs, MOF-Verify substantially improves hypothesis-verification performance over direct inference and retrieval-based baselines. Benchmark datasets are released at https://github.com/IMMS-Ewha/MOF-Verify-Benchmark.

</details>


### [42] [Peer Influence across Heterogeneous AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03095)
*Frida Nøhr Laustsen,Marie Haahr Petersen,Victoria Popa,Ariel Flint,Romualdo Pastor-Satorras,Andrea Baronchelli,Luca Maria Aiello*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 30 pages, 16 Figures, 6 Tables

Selection reason: 研究异构 LLM 多智能体间说服与判断偏移，揭示模型组合层面的可靠性问题，对多 agent 协作与验证有启发。

TL;DR: 研究测量AI智能体间单轮交流后的说服效应，测试7个开放权重模型、3个语言理解任务，发现说服强烈且不可由模型规模或单独确定性预测，主要取决于听者易受说服性，并具有配对特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统常混合不同语言模型，分歧时谁影响谁决定交互后判断，需理解说服动态。

Method: 将说服定义为单次与异议同伴交流后决策的概率偏移；在三个语言理解任务上测试七个开放权重模型。

Result: 说服效应强，接收者常放弃初始判断；单独确定性与规模不能可靠预测；小模型可匹敌大模型作为说服者并有效抵抗；偏移更取决于听者易受性；说服模式配对特异，异质性可放大或抑制，甚至小模型可推翻大模型判断。

Conclusion: 交互模型行为不能由个体属性推断，需在其实际组合中评估。

中文摘要：当两个AI智能体意见不一致时，谁会说服谁？随着多智能体系统日益结合不同家族和不同规模的语言模型，答案可能决定哪些判断能在交互后存活。我们将说服度量为一个智能体在与持不同意见的同伴进行单次交流后其决策发生的概率性偏移，并在三个语言理解任务上测试了七个开放权重模型。我们发现说服效应很强：当模型意见不一致时，接收者常在看到同伴的答案和解释后放弃其初始判断。然而，出乎意料的是，无论是单独情况下的确定性还是模型规模，都不能可靠预测说服动态。在孤立情况下产生几乎完全一致决策的模型，可能属于最容易受说服影响的模型之列；而小模型作为说服者时可以匹敌更大的模型，并且在抵抗其影响方面也同样有效。此外，我们表明，偏移的大小更多取决于听者的易受说服性，而非说者的说服力。因此，说服模式特定于每个模型配对，异质性在一些组合中放大说服，在另一些组合中抑制说服，使得一个运行小模型的持不同意见智能体能够推翻一个大得多的模型的判断。这些发现表明，交互模型的行为不能从其个体属性推断，而必须在它们将运行的组合中进行评估。

Original abstract: When two AI agents disagree, who persuades whom? As multi-agent systems increasingly combine language models of different families and sizes, the answer can determine which judgments survive interaction. Measuring persuasion as the probabilistic shift in an agent's decision after a single exchange with a dissenting peer, we test seven open-weight models across three language understanding tasks. We find that persuasion is strong: when models disagree, receivers often abandon their initial judgment after seeing a peer's answer and explanation. Surprisingly, however, neither standalone certainty nor model scale reliably predicts persuasion dynamics. Models producing almost perfectly consistent decisions in isolation can be among the most susceptible to persuasion, and small models can match larger ones as persuaders and resist their influence just as effectively. Furthermore, we show that the size of the shift depends more on the susceptibility of the listener than on the persuasiveness of the speaker. Persuasion patterns are therefore specific to each model pairing, with heterogeneity amplifying persuasion in some combinations and suppressing it in others, allowing a dissenting agent running a small model to overturn the judgments of a much larger one. These findings show that the behavior of interacting models cannot be inferred from their individual properties but must be evaluated in the combinations in which they will operate.

</details>


### [43] [Trading Strategy Optimization via Textual Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03128)
*Chaoqun Yang,Qian Wang,Fengbin Zhu,Xinyu Lin,Bingsheng He,Roger Zimmermann,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将 LLM textual gradient 用于交易策略程序的黑箱优化，含经验复用与多尺度修正，方法可迁移到自动代码优化。

TL;DR: 提出TradeGrad，利用经验引导文本梯度与多尺度修正优化交易策略，并引入跨期稳健目标CPRO，提升时间稳健性；在A股和美股四类任务中取得最佳样本内外表现，中国横截面策略年化27.99%、最大回撤12.19%、夏普1.63。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接使用LLM文本梯度进行量化策略优化存在优化短视、未充分利用历史评估经验，以及聚合回测反馈忽视时间稳健性、偏向特定时期表现好的策略等问题。

Method: TradeGrad：经验引导文本梯度框架，利用累积优化经验估计文本梯度，对策略探索与精炼进行多尺度修正，并引入跨期稳健目标CPRO，侧重不利历史时期表现以提升时间稳健性。

Result: 在中国A股与美国股票市场的横截面与时间序列策略设计四类设置中，TradeGrad样本内和样本外表现均最优；中国横截面策略年化收益27.99%，最大回撤12.19%，夏普比率1.63，较沪深300基准约高68%；进一步分析验证组件有效性，优化过程中样本内外表现持续改善。代码开源。

Conclusion: TradeGrad通过经验引导文本梯度和跨期稳健目标，有效缓解优化短视与时间稳健性不足问题，实现稳健且跨市场有效的交易策略优化。

中文摘要：量化交易策略设计旨在从历史数据中发现未来市场中仍有效的交易程序，可视为一个黑盒程序优化问题。基于LLM的文本梯度通过为迭代策略优化提供显式优化方向，提供了一种有前景的方法。然而，直接应用文本梯度面临两个挑战：(1)优化是短视的，未充分利用先前评估中的经验；(2)聚合回测反馈忽视了时间稳健性，可能偏向于仅在某些特定市场时期表现良好的策略。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了TradeGrad，一个用于稳健交易策略优化的经验引导文本梯度框架。TradeGrad利用积累的优化经验来估计文本梯度，并采用多尺度修正用于策略探索和优化。它进一步引入了跨期稳健目标（Cross-Period Robust Objective, CPRO），该目标强调在不利历史时期的表现，以促进时间稳健性。在中国A股和美国股票市场进行的横截面和时间序列策略设计实验表明，TradeGrad在所有四种设置中均实现了最佳的样本内和样本外表现。值得注意的是，其中国横截面策略实现了27.99%的年化收益、12.19%的最大回撤和1.63的夏普比率，约比沪深300基准高68%。进一步分析验证了所提出的组件，并显示在整个优化过程中样本内和样本外表现持续改善。代码可在 https://github.com/transcend-0/TradeGrad 获取。

Original abstract: Quantitative trading strategy design aims to discover trading programs from historical data that remain effective in future markets, which can be viewed as a black-box program optimization problem. LLM-based textual gradients offer a promising approach by providing explicit optimization directions for iterative strategy refinement. However, directly applying textual gradients faces two challenges: (1) optimization is myopic, underutilizing experience from previous evaluations; and (2) aggregate backtest feedback overlooks temporal robustness, potentially favoring strategies that perform well only in specific market periods. To address these challenges, we propose TradeGrad, an experience-guided textual-gradient framework for robust trading strategy optimization. TradeGrad leverages accumulated optimization experience to estimate textual gradients and employs multi-scale revisions for both strategy exploration and refinement. It further introduces the Cross-Period Robust Objective (CPRO), which emphasizes performance in unfavorable historical periods to promote temporal robustness. Experiments on cross-sectional and time-series strategy design in Chinese A-share and U.S. equity markets show that TradeGrad achieves the best in-sample and out-of-sample performance across all four settings. Notably, its Chinese cross-sectional strategy achieves 27.99% annualized return, 12.19% maximum drawdown, and a Sharpe ratio of 1.63, approximately 68% higher than the CSI 300 benchmark. Further analyses validate the proposed components and show consistent improvements in both in-sample and out-of-sample performance throughout optimization. The code is available at https://github.com/transcend-0/TradeGrad.

</details>


### [44] [Toward SLM-based agentic task-tool intent matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03213)
*Chiara Troiani,Arash Salarian,Majed El Helou,Benjamin Ryder,Jean Diaconu,Hervé Muyal,Marcelo Yannuzzi*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向 tool-equipped agent 的每次工具调用意图匹配与 SLM 监控，属于工具使用安全控制与验证。

TL;DR: 研究用小语言模型作为逐调用任务-工具相关性分类器，并借助跨MCP服务器的多工具任务数据集，通过提示优化、监督微调和GRPO强化学习进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统横向扩展使工具调用交互激增，需要低延迟、可本地部署的自动化逐调用监督；传统授权只能判断是否允许调用，无法判断调用是否符合任务意图，被允许的调用仍可能被失控智能体用于偏离任务或诱导其他智能体形成偏离任务意图的调用组合。

Method: 将SLM用作任务-工具相关性分类器，对每次选中的工具独立评估其与给定任务的相关性，输出供下游执行使用的相关性信号；构建所需工具跨越不同MCP服务器的多工具任务数据集；采用提示优化、监督微调以及通过GRPO实现的强化学习来优化和专门化SLM。

Result: 摘要未报告具体性能数值；工作产出包括提出SLM逐调用任务-工具相关性分类方案、构建跨MCP服务器的多工具任务数据集，并完成基于提示优化、监督微调和GRPO的SLM优化与专门化。

Conclusion: 该研究探索SLM用于自动化逐调用监督的适用性，以在传统授权之外补充任务意图层面的验证；摘要未给出最终有效性结论或部署效果，仍需进一步实验评估。

中文摘要：配备工具的AI智能体通过工具调用来访问数据并对接外部系统。智能体系统的横向扩展增加了这些交互的数量，并进一步推动了对于能够以低延迟和/或本地部署方式运行的自动化逐调用监督的需求。传统授权方案可以判断智能体是否被允许调用某个工具，但无法评估智能体的底层认知，具体而言，无法评估工具选择是否代表朝着满足任务意图迈出的符合逻辑且相关的步骤。因此，一次被允许的调用仍可能偏离任务意图：失控智能体可能使调用偏离，或诱导其他智能体进行不符合任务意图的调用组合。所以，每一次调用都需要被验证。在本研究中，我们探讨小型语言模型（SLM）用于这一目的的适用性：SLM充当任务-工具相关性分类器，针对所分配任务独立评估每一个被选中的工具，并返回相关性信号以供下游执行。借助一个包含多工具任务的新数据集，这些任务所需工具跨越不同的模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器，我们使用提示优化、监督微调和通过GRPO实现的强化学习来优化并专门化SLM。

Original abstract: Tool-equipped AI agents use tool calls to access data and act on external systems. Horizontal growth of agentic systems increases the number of these interactions, and further motivates the need for automated, per-call oversight that can operate at low latency and/or on-prem. Conventional authorization schemes can determine whether an agent is allowed to invoke a tool, but cannot assess the agent's underlying cognition, specifically, whether the tool selection represents a logical, relevant step toward satisfying the intent of the task or not. Consequently, an allowed call may still deviate from the task's intent: a rogue agent might deviate the calls or nudge other agents to make a combination of calls that would not align with the intent of the task. Therefore, every call needs to be verified. In this study we investigate the applicability of Small Language Models (SLMs) to this purpose: an SLM functions as a task-tool relevance classifier that evaluates every selected tool independently against the assigned task and returns a relevance signal for downstream enforcement. Equipped with a novel dataset with multi-tool tasks whose required tools span distinct Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, we used prompt-optimization, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning through GRPO to optimize and specialize SLMs.

</details>


### [45] [JOVE: Joint Execution and Verification for Resource-Aware LLM Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03296)
*Haoran Zhang,Dongjun Kim,Seohyeon Cha,Kevin S Chan,Ananthram Swami,Gustavo De Veciana,Haris Vikalo*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: preprint

Selection reason: 对 LLM 任务图进行执行者分配与付费验证的在线联合优化，涉及 agent workflow、验证预算与学习，明显可迁移。

TL;DR: JOVE是一个在线框架，通过联合分配异构LLM执行器并异步付费验证中间输出，在长期预算与每查询延迟约束下平衡执行开销与学习收益，从而提升复杂推理任务的成本、延迟与准确率表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂推理查询可分解为任务图并并行分配给异构LLM，但LLM对特定子任务的适用性往往先验未知，且执行结果本身不能揭示正确性，因此需要在线决策执行与验证，以在预算和延迟约束下优化长期性能。

Method: JOVE将执行器分配与中间输出付费验证建模为每查询的混合整数线性规划序列；验证异步进行并反馈用于在线学习，更新任务相关的LLM质量估计；同时在分配决策中加入信息增益奖励，以权衡当前执行开销与未来学习价值。

Result: 在四个推理基准上，JOVE相对标准推理基线取得有竞争力的准确率，并将平均成本和延迟至少降低3.17倍；理论上在自然假设下建立了次线性质量学习遗憾。

Conclusion: JOVE表明，将在线学习、信息增益驱动的分配与异步付费验证结合，可在未知且随机的LLM服务质量、调用成本和执行时间下，有效优化长期预算与延迟约束下的复杂推理执行。}

中文摘要：复杂推理查询可以分解为有向无环任务图，并分布到异构LLM上执行，通过并行化降低延迟，并使较小模型能够解决复杂任务。然而在实践中，一个LLM对于给定子任务的适用性可能是先验未知的，并且仅靠执行并不能揭示输出正确性。我们提出JOVE，一个在线框架，它联合分配执行器LLM并选择中间输出进行付费验证。验证异步运行，并用于改进未来的分配，因此系统必须在当前执行开销与后续学习之间进行权衡。我们研究如何在长期预算和每查询延迟约束下优化这一权衡，其中LLM服务质量、调用成本和执行时间均为随机且初始未知。JOVE通过求解一系列每查询混合整数线性规划来做出执行和验证决策。在线学习基于验证反馈更新任务相关的LLM质量估计，而信息增益奖励将学习的价值纳入分配决策。在一组自然假设下，我们为JOVE建立了次线性质量学习遗憾。在四个推理基准上，JOVE相对于标准推理基线取得了有竞争力的准确率，同时将平均成本和延迟至少降低3.17倍。

Original abstract: Complex reasoning queries can be decomposed into directed acyclic task graphs and distributed across heterogeneous LLMs, reducing latency through parallelism and enabling smaller models to solve complex tasks. In practice, however, the suitability of an LLM for a given subtask may be a priori unknown, and execution alone does not reveal output correctness. We propose JOVE, an online framework that jointly assigns executor LLMs and selects intermediate outputs for paid verification. Verification runs asynchronously and is used to improve future allocations, so the system must balance spending on execution now against learning for later. We study how to optimize this trade-off under a long-term budget and a per-query latency constraint, with stochastic, initially unknown LLM service quality, invocation costs, and execution times. JOVE makes execution and verification decisions by solving a sequence of per-query mixed-integer linear programs. Online learning updates task-dependent estimates of LLM quality based on verification feedback, while an information-gain bonus incorporates the value of learning into allocation decisions. Under a natural set of assumptions, we establish sublinear quality-learning regret for JOVE. Across four reasoning benchmarks, JOVE achieves competitive accuracy against standard inference baselines while reducing average cost and latency by at least 3.17 times.

</details>


### [46] [Lightweight, Rubric-Guided Trajectory Evaluation for Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03315)
*Linh-An Phan,MingXue Wang,Guangyu Wu,Feng Pan,Zhaoyu Pang,Yanbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向生产 LLM agent 的轻量轨迹评估与失败定位，直接相关 agent 评测与可靠性。

TL;DR: 提出轻量级预算受限轨迹评估架构LiteTrajEval，通过离线规则配置、在线预处理、启发式失败信号、固定预算序列化和单次评分规则引导的LLM评判，显著提升失败定位一致性并降低成本与时间，且已部署于企业智能体平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹评估对提高基于LLM的智能体可靠性必不可少，但生产环境中反复评估成本高；同时现代智能体轨迹很长，包含工具调用、观察、重试和外部输出，原始token对诊断的价值并不均匀。

Method: LiteTrajEval离线推导紧凑的领域特定规则配置文件；在线预处理每条轨迹，标记启发式失败信号，并在固定全局预算下序列化；随后调用单个由评分规则引导的LLM评判器，生成结构化诊断报告。

Result: 在公开的Magentic-One风格和τ-bench风格轨迹数据集上，相比AgentRx，LiteTrajEval在Magentic-One上将失败定位与人工标注的一致性提高约20–35个百分点，在τ-retail上最多提高23个百分点，同时成本降低约6倍，评估时间缩短超过8倍。

Conclusion: LiteTrajEval是一种有效的预算受限轨迹评估方案，能在提升失败定位诊断质量的同时显著降低成本和时间，并已实际部署于企业智能体平台。

中文摘要：轨迹评估对于提升基于LLM的智能体可靠性至关重要，但在生产使用中反复运行成本高昂。现代智能体会生成包含工具调用、观察、重试和外部输出的长轨迹，而并非所有原始token对诊断都同等有用。我们提出LiteTrajEval，一种用于预算受限轨迹评估的轻量级架构。LiteTrajEval离线推导紧凑的领域特定规则配置文件，然后在线预处理每条轨迹，标记启发式失败信号，在固定全局预算下将其序列化，并调用单个由评分规则引导的LLM评判器生成结构化诊断报告。在公开的Magentic-One风格和τ-bench风格轨迹数据集上评估，与AgentRx相比，LiteTrajEval在Magentic-One上使失败定位与人工标注的一致性提高约20–35个百分点，在τ-retail上提高最多23个百分点，同时将成本降低约6倍，评估时间缩短超过8倍。该方案也已部署在我们的企业智能体平台中。

Original abstract: Trajectory evaluation is essential for improving the reliability of LLM-based agents, but production use makes it expensive to run repeatedly. Modern agents generate long traces containing tool calls, observations, retries, and external outputs, while not all raw tokens are equally useful for diagnosis. We present \textit{LiteTrajEval}, a lightweight architecture for budget-bounded trajectory evaluation. LiteTrajEval derives compact domain-specific rule profiles offline, then preprocesses each trajectory online, marks heuristic failure signals, serializes it under a fixed global budget, and invokes a single rubric-guided LLM judge to produce structured diagnostic reports. Evaluated on public Magentic-One-style and $τ$-bench-style trajectory datasets, LiteTrajEval improves failure-localization alignment with human annotations by roughly 20--35 percentage points on Magentic-One and up to 23 percentage points on $τ$-retail compared with AgentRx, while reducing cost by about 6$\times$ and evaluation time by more than 8$\times$. This solution has also been deployed in our enterprise agentic platform.

</details>


### [47] [Multi-Task Evolution for Zero-Shot Cross-Problem Generalization using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03316)
*Zhouliang Xie,Changliang Zhou,Genghui Li,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM 驱动的多任务进化生成启发式并跨问题零样本泛化，属于 LLM 自动程序/启发式优化，可迁移到代码优化。

TL;DR: 提出 MECo：一种 LLM 驱动的多任务进化框架，通过任务条件启发式种群、跨任务迁移差距引导交互以及覆盖度选择，实现零样本跨问题启发式泛化；在 VRP 和 FJSP 的 32 个变体上优于八个 AHD 基线，并提升不同 AHD 方法的 ID/OOD 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为不同组合优化问题设计启发式需要专家知识和反复搜索；现有 LLM 可自动生成和改进启发式，但搜索通常依赖目标问题的评估反馈，难以仅凭源任务反馈泛化到新问题定义。

Method: MECo 维护任务条件启发式种群，以跨任务种群性能定义的迁移差距引导种群交互，实现启发式迁移与重组；再用互补选择准则奖励成员对源组合的额外覆盖，构建紧凑启发式集合，并直接用于目标问题而不再搜索或适应。

Result: 在 VRP 和 FJSP 的 32 个问题变体上，相同预算下 MECo 的平均成本低于八个 AHD 基线；在域外问题上，它在每个问题族中均超过最强基线；将 MECo 与不同 AHD 方法集成还能提升两个问题族的 ID 和 OOD 性能。

Conclusion: MECo 能有效实现零样本跨问题启发式泛化，并可作为通用框架增强不同自动启发式设计方法的域内与域外表现。

中文摘要：为多样化的组合优化问题设计有效的启发式算法需要大量专业知识和反复搜索。大语言模型（LLM）能够自动生成和改进启发式，但启发式搜索通常依赖于来自被优化问题的评估反馈。因此，仅使用源任务反馈来泛化到新的问题定义仍然是一个核心挑战。我们提出了 MECo，一种由 LLM 驱动的多任务进化框架，用于零样本跨问题泛化。MECo 维护以任务为条件的启发式种群，并使用基于跨任务种群性能的迁移差距来引导它们之间的交互。这些交互使启发式能够迁移和重组。随后，一个互补的选择准则通过奖励每个成员对源问题组合的额外覆盖，构建一个紧凑的启发式集合。所选集合被应用于目标问题，无需进一步搜索或适应。在车辆路径问题（VRP）和柔性作业车间调度（FJSP）的 32 个问题变体上的实验表明，在相同预算下，与八个自动启发式设计（AHD）基线相比，MECo 取得了最低的平均成本。在域外问题上，它在每个问题族中都优于最强基线。此外，将 MECo 框架与不同 AHD 方法集成，可提升它们在两个问题族中的域内（ID）和域外（OOD）性能，这表明其在不同方法上均有效。

Original abstract: Designing effective heuristics for diverse combinatorial optimization problems requires substantial expertise and repeated search. Large language models (LLMs) automate heuristic generation and refinement, but heuristic search typically depends on evaluation feedback from the problem being optimized. Generalizing to new problem definitions using only source-task feedback therefore remains a central challenge. We introduce MECo, an LLM-driven multi-task evolutionary framework for zero-shot cross-problem generalization. MECo maintains task-conditioned heuristic populations and uses a transfer gap based on cross-task population performance to guide their interactions. These interactions enable the transfer and recombination of heuristics. A complementary selection criterion then constructs a compact heuristic set by rewarding each member's additional coverage of source combinations. The selected set is applied to target problems without further search or adaptation. Experiments on 32 problem variants across vehicle routing (VRP) and flexible job-shop scheduling (FJSP) show that MECo achieves the lowest mean costs compared with eight automated heuristic design (AHD) baselines under the same budgets. On out-of-domain problems, it outperforms the strongest baseline in each family. Moreover, integrating the framework of MECo with different AHD methods improves their ID and OOD performance in both families, supporting its effectiveness across different methods.

</details>


### [48] [ReFract: Benchmarking Perspective Awareness in Language Model Agents with Text World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03356)
*Hainiu Xu,Vítor N. Lourenço,Mohnish Dubey,Yunfei Bai,Yulan He,Caroline Catmur,Aline Paes,Marco Caserta,Akash Chandrayan,Luca D'Angelo*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向 LLM agent 的角色视角感知 benchmark，考察按用户角色合法使用工具与动作边界，可迁移至 agent 可靠性评估。

TL;DR: 提出 ReFract 基准，评估 LLM 智能体能否根据用户角色调整行动；包含 150 个专家验证任务，SOTA 最高解决 69%，超 50% 轨迹出现视角违规尝试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险工业维护与设备故障排查中，智能体必须依据用户角色校准行动，避免越权或提供不适当信息；现有基准忽视这种视角意识，而错误可能造成不可逆后果。

Method: 构建 ReFract：150 个专家验证条目，基于匿名领域支持对话，构建文本世界模型模拟操作环境，并组装视角感知动作轨迹；同一查询因用户角色不同而要求不同行动。

Result: 最先进 LLM 最多解决 69% 的任务，且超过 50% 的轨迹包含违反视角的行动尝试；视角意识成为智能体评估中一个独立且基本未解决的维度。

Conclusion: 视角意识是智能体评估中尚未解决的重要能力轴，未来智能体不仅需校准如何行动，还需校准为谁行动。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体正越来越多地部署在工业维护和设备故障排查等高风险场景中，在这些场景里，工人承担着多种角色。因此，一个有能力智能体必须以与用户角色相校准的方式行动：所采取的行动和提供的信息应尊重该角色的知识与能力边界。与编码不同，编码中的错误通常可恢复，而在此类场景中，智能体响应会作用于物理设备，因而可能导致不可逆的设备损坏、生产损失或人员伤害。然而，现有基准在很大程度上忽视了智能体需要推断某个角色意图什么，并且仅通过该角色可合法使用的工具来行动，我们将这一能力称为视角意识（Perspective Awareness）。为此，我们提出 ReFract，一个包含 150 个经专家验证条目的基准，其中智能体必须根据用户角色对同一查询做出不同响应。ReFract 的条目基于来自领域支持对话的匿名化查询，我们据此构建模拟智能体操作环境的文本世界模型（Text World Models），并组装视角感知的动作轨迹。最先进的 LLM 最多解决 69% 的任务，且其超过 50% 的轨迹包含采取违反视角行动的尝试。ReFract 将视角意识揭示为智能体评估中一个独特的、基本上尚未解决的维度，并推动智能体不仅校准如何行动，还要校准为谁行动。

Original abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as industrial maintenance and equipment fault troubleshooting, where workers occupy a variety of roles. A capable agent must therefore act in a way that is calibrated to user's role: taking actions and providing information that respect the role's knowledge and capability boundaries. Unlike coding, where mistakes are usually recoverable, agent responses in these settings are enacted on physical equipment, and can therefore cause irreversible equipment damage, production loss, or personnel harm. Existing benchmarks, however, largely overlook the need for agents to infer what a role intends and acting only through tools that role may legitimately use, a capability which we term Perspective Awareness. To this end, we introduce ReFract, a benchmark of 150 expert-validated entries in which an agent must act differently in response to the same query depending on user's role. Entries of ReFract are grounded in anonymized queries from domain support conversations, against which we construct Text World Models that simulate the agent's operating environments and assemble perspective-aware action trajectories. State-of-the-art LLMs solve at most 69% of the tasks with more than 50% of their trajectories contain attempts of taking perspective-violating actions. ReFract exposes perspective awareness as a distinct, largely unsolved axis of agent evaluation and motivates agents that calibrate not just how to act, but for whom.

</details>


### [49] [CVE2AP: Automated Generation of PDDL-Encoded Attack Paths via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03383)
*Lin Cui,Vincenzo Scotti,Raffaela Mirandola*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM 生成 PDDL 攻击路径并利用规划器错误反馈迭代修正，具备工具反馈与形式验证机制，对 agent 工具使用有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出CVE2AP，利用LLM从CVE描述自动生成PDDL攻击路径，通过结构化提示和错误反馈迭代修正；多LLM评估显示语法、可解性和语义正确率最高达86.9%、78.6%和93.1%，GPT-5.5质量-成本最优，错误反馈提升最稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有攻击路径建模依赖专家手工构建，可扩展性差，难以跟上快速演变的网络威胁；LLM具备广泛预训练知识和推理能力，有望自动将威胁情报转化为形式化表示。

Method: CVE2AP基于LLM，从自然语言CVE描述生成PDDL编码攻击路径；采用结构化提示，并引入基于规划器报告的语法错误和可解性错误的迭代反馈精化机制；在多个LLM和生成配置上进行系统实证评估，考察语法、可解性、语义质量以及token消耗和生成时间。

Result: CVE2AP生成的PDDL攻击路径最高达到86.9%语法正确率、78.6%可解性和93.1%语义正确率（LLM作为专家评估）；GPT-5.5提供最佳质量-成本权衡，错误反馈带来最一致的质量提升。

Conclusion: CVE2AP能有效自动生成高质量的PDDL编码攻击路径，表明LLM结合结构化提示和错误反馈可提升攻击路径形式化建模的自动化与可扩展性，其中模型选择和反馈机制对质量与成本具有重要影响。

中文摘要：攻击路径（AP）建模是网络安全分析的基础，其中规划领域定义语言（PDDL）已被广泛采用，用于将AP编码为形式化且机器可验证的表示，以对漏洞利用、攻击进展及其潜在影响进行自动推理。然而，现有的AP建模方法在很大程度上依赖专家驱动的手动构建，限制了其可扩展性以及跟上快速演变的网络威胁的能力。大型语言模型（LLM）是有前景的候选方案，因为它们广泛的预训练知识和推理能力使其能够解释威胁情报并将其转换为形式化表示。在本文中，我们提出CVE2AP，一种基于LLM的方法，用于从自然语言CVE（通用漏洞披露）描述中自动生成PDDL编码的攻击路径。CVE2AP利用结构化提示，并引入错误反馈机制，该机制利用规划器报告的语法和可解性错误迭代地精化生成的路径。我们在多个LLM和生成配置上进行了系统性的实证评估，从语法、可解性和语义维度评估生成质量，并同时评估token消耗和生成时间。结果表明，CVE2AP能有效生成高质量的PDDL编码攻击路径，在LLM作为专家评估下，分别达到高达86.9%的语法正确率、78.6%的可解性和93.1%的语义正确率，同时GPT-5.5提供了最佳的质量-成本权衡，而错误反馈带来了最一致的质量提升。

Original abstract: Attack Path (AP) modeling is fundamental to cybersecurity analysis, where the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) has been widely adopted to encode APs into formal and machine-verifiable representations for automated reasoning about vulnerability exploitation, attack progression, and their potential impacts. However, existing AP modeling approaches largely rely on expert-driven manual construction, limiting their scalability and ability to keep pace with rapidly evolving cyber threats. Large language models (LLMs) are promising candidates, as their extensive pre-trained knowledge and reasoning capabilities enable them to interpret and transform threat intelligence into formal representations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CVE2AP}, an LLM-based approach for automatically generating PDDL-encoded attack paths from natural language CVE (Common Vulnerability Exposure) descriptions. CVE2AP leverages structured prompting and incorporates an error-feedback mechanism that iteratively refines the generated paths using planner-reported syntactic and solvability errors. We conduct a systematic empirical evaluation across multiple LLMs and generation configurations, assessing generation quality across syntactic, solvability and semantic dimensions, together with token consumption and generation time. The results demonstrate that CVE2AP effectively generates high-quality PDDL-encoded attack paths, achieving up to 86.9\% syntax correctness, 78.6\% solvability, and 93.1\% semantic correctness under LLM-as-expert evaluation, while \texttt{GPT-5.5} offers the best quality-cost trade-off and error feedback yields the most consistent quality improvement.

</details>


### [50] [A Near-Zero Monitor Readout Is Not Evidence of Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03458)
*Zhe Zhou,Tianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 2 figures, 10 tables. Accepted as a poster at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Foundations of LLM Post-Training in Changing Environments (FLLMPT)

Selection reason: 在代码生成环境中研究 monitor 低读数与行为控制、奖励 hacking，对 coding agent 的可靠监控有启发。

TL;DR: 低监控器读数不能证明训练中的干预已控制行为；相同零中位训练分数下，仅因随机种子不同，前缀训练运行可从低黑客占比变为近乎纯粹的奖励黑客行为，因此需要带外行为检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励后训练会诱发奖励黑客行为，因此希望将监控器纳入训练目标而非仅用于离线审计；但低监控读数是否意味着行为已受控仍不明确。

Method: 在代码生成环境中，主要漏洞在推理轨迹开始时即可用；针对三个通过相同离线门槛的监控器训练策略：域内激活探针，以及两个以策略多早承诺最终答案为条件的惩罚项。比较不同随机种子和前缀训练运行的端点读数，并进行文本层面分析。

Result: 探针分数从首个记录训练步骤起即处于数值下限，前缀训练运行在端点处的训练分数中位数均为零；但在同一固定配置下，仅因随机种子不同，运行可从低黑客占比的混合状态变为近乎纯粹的奖励黑客行为。所有探针运行进入黑客行为状态，其下限水平读数反映探针验证位置与训练读取位置不匹配。文本分析发现前缀失败模式：通用规划和填充外壳将漏洞利用推迟到截断之后，却未从最终输出中消除。低测得承诺不能区分低黑客占比与延迟利用。

Conclusion: 离线判别和低监控器对齐读数不足以证明行为控制；必须进行带外行为检查。本文刻画的是端点读数而非其演化过程。代码见 https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost。

中文摘要：使用可验证奖励进行后训练可能诱发奖励黑客行为，这促使人们在训练目标中使用监控器，而不仅仅用于离线审计。我们表明，较低的监控器读数并不能识别这种干预是否控制了行为。在一个代码生成环境中，其主要可利用漏洞在推理轨迹开始时即可用，我们针对三个通过相同离线门槛的监控器训练策略：一个域内激活探针，以及两个以策略多早承诺其自身最终答案为条件的惩罚项。探针分数从第一个记录的训练步骤起就处于其数值下限，并且在端点处，每个前缀训练运行的训练分数中位数都为零。这些读数估计的是不同的量，我们也不比较它们的尺度；然而，在每一个监控器家族内，低值并不能确立行为控制。在一个固定配置内，具有相同零中位数训练分数的前缀训练运行，仅因随机种子不同，就从低黑客比例的混合状态到近乎纯粹奖励黑客行为之间变化。所有探针运行都达到黑客行为状态，但其处于下限水平的读数反映的是探针被验证的位置与其在训练期间被读取的位置之间的不匹配，而不是这种模糊性的第二个实例。文本层面的分析识别出一种前缀失败模式：通用规划和填充外壳将漏洞利用推迟到截断之后，却没有将其从最终输出中消除。因此，测得的低承诺程度不能区分低黑客比例与对漏洞利用的延迟承诺。离线判别和低监控器对齐读数不足以证明行为控制；需要带外行为检查。我们刻画的是端点读数，而不是其演化过程。代码可在 https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost 获取。

Original abstract: Post-training with verifiable rewards can induce reward hacking, motivating the use of monitors within the training objective rather than solely for offline auditing. We show that a low monitor readout does not identify whether such an intervention controls behavior. In a code-generation environment whose dominant exploit is available at the start of the reasoning trace, we train policies against three monitors that pass the same offline gate: an in-domain activation probe and two penalties conditioned on how early the policy commits to its own final answer. The probe score is at its numerical floor from the first recorded training step, and the trained-score median is zero for every prefix-trained run at the endpoint. These readouts estimate different quantities, and we do not compare their scales; within each monitor family, however, low values do not establish behavioral control. Within one fixed configuration, prefix-trained runs with the same zero-median trained score range, by seed alone, from a mixed regime with a low hacking share to near-pure reward hacking. All probe runs reach the hacking regime, but their floor-level readout reflects a mismatch between the position where the probe was validated and the position where it was read during training, not a second instance of this ambiguity. Text-level analysis identifies a prefix failure mode: generic planning and filler shells postpone the exploit past the cut without eliminating it from the final output. Low measured commitment therefore does not distinguish a low hacking share from delayed commitment to the exploit. Offline discrimination and low monitor-aligned readouts are insufficient evidence of behavioral control; an out-of-band behavioral check is required. We characterize the endpoint readout, not its evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost.

</details>


### [51] [From Benchmarks to Production: A Text-to-SQL System for Complex Financial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03524)
*Arijit Sehanobish,Bruno Gomes Coelho,Guillaume Michel,Sophia Zhi,Valerie Faucon-Morin,Kristen Howell*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: EMNLP Industry Track 2026

Selection reason: 生产级 Text-to-SQL 系统含 lookup agent、模板检索与 schema linking，方法可迁移至代码/SQL agent。

TL;DR: FLINT是面向生产金融数据库的领域专用Text-to-SQL系统，通过查找代理、模板检索和外键链模式链接，解决不透明ID和复杂连接导致的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用Text-to-SQL在学术基准表现好，但生产金融数据库中概念常以不透明整数键存储，列值不可读，简单查询也需多连接且过滤引用不透明ID，导致现有方法准确率低于50%。

Method: FLINT包含三个组件：动态将自然语言概念解析为问题特定参考表约束的查找代理；从紧凑专家编写库中基于嵌入检索结构相似查询模板；通过遍历外键链而非仅名称相似性来裁剪大表模式的相关子集。

Result: 在两个共359个问题的生产金融模式数据集上评估，FLINT在相同LLM下优于多种最先进基线，并已作为金融数据检索服务的一部分部署于生产环境。

Conclusion: 领域专用设计可有效弥合通用Text-to-SQL在生产金融数据库上的差距，并具备实际部署价值。

中文摘要：通用Text-to-SQL系统在Spider和BIRD等学术基准上取得了强劲性能，这些基准中的模式相对较浅，且列值通常人类可读。在生产金融数据库中，概念以不透明的整数键而非人类可读字符串存储，这些方法的表现低于50%，因为即使简单查询也需要多次连接，且过滤谓词引用不透明ID。我们提出Financial LINking Text-to-SQL（FLINT），一种领域专用的Text-to-SQL系统，通过三个关键组件弥合这一差距：（1）一个查找代理，动态将自然语言概念解析为特定问题的参考表约束；（2）基于嵌入的检索，从紧凑的专家编写库中检索结构相似的查询模板；（3）模式链接，通过遍历外键链将大表模式裁剪到相关子集，而不是仅依赖名称相似性。我们在两个数据集上评估，共359个问题，基于生产金融模式。FLINT在相同LLM下优于各种最先进基线。该系统已部署于生产环境，作为金融数据检索服务的一部分。

Original abstract: General-purpose Text-to-SQL systems achieve strong performance on academic benchmarks like Spider and BIRD, where schemas are relatively shallow and column values are often human readable. In production financial databases, where concepts are stored as opaque integer keys rather than human-readable strings, these methods fall below 50%, as even simple queries require multiple joins and filter predicates reference opaque IDs. We present Financial LINking Text-to-SQL (FLINT), a domain-specialized Text-to-SQL system that closes this gap through three key components: (1) a lookup agent that dynamically resolves natural-language concepts to question-specific reference table constraints, (2) embedding-based retrieval of structurally similar query templates from a compact, expert-authored bank, and (3) schema linking that prunes a large table schema to the relevant subset by traversing foreign-key chains, rather than relying on name similarity alone. We evaluate on two datasets totaling 359 questions over production financial schemas. FLINT outperforms various state-of-the-art baselines using the same LLM. The system is deployed in production as part of a financial data retrieval service.

</details>


### [52] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Frontier Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03548)
*Wenlong Zhang,Zhengbo Jiao,Chenxu Zhang,Lekang Jiang,SiYuan Ma,Qituan Zhang,Guo Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 递归 harness 自改进与 task-harness 协同进化，直接关联 agent harness、工作流自优化与经验复用。

TL;DR: 提出任务-框架协同演化框架，通过在线与任务后自我改进递归提升推理数据合成难度；在数学、编程和科学上使求解器平均准确率从100.0%降至54.8%，提升下游SFT和GRPO，27B模型在APEX达62.5% mean-16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务级递归只复用生成问题作为种子，构建框架不变，无法随任务分布演变而调整合成过程，因此需要递归地自我改进框架，以生成逐步更难的推理问题。

Method: 提出任务-框架协同演化，用于推理数据合成中的递归式框架自我改进（RSI）。在线自我改进在生成中将求解器中间失败转为可复用技能；任务后自我改进在每批后修订技能、提示和工作流，仅当候选能在成本增幅有界内生成更难合法任务时采纳。模型权重与验证标准固定。

Result: 在数学、编程和科学中，经过14轮演化，求解器平均准确率从100.0%降至54.8%。消融显示两种更新计划结合比固定框架递归或任一单独计划生成更难任务。所得数据提升下游SFT和GRPO；27B学生模型用1万条合成数学示例微调后在APEX达62.5% mean-16，与选定前沿模型参考结果相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在调整任务的同时调整合成框架，能够生成日益困难且具有下游训练价值的数据。

中文摘要：生成逐步更难的推理问题需要能够随着任务分布演变而调整的合成过程。现有的任务级递归将生成的问题作为种子复用，但保持构建框架不变。我们提出任务-框架协同演化，一个用于推理数据合成中递归式框架自我改进（RSI）的框架。在线自我改进在生成过程中将求解器的中间失败转化为可复用技能。任务后自我改进在每批之后修订技能、提示和工作流，仅当候选方案能在成本增幅有界的情况下生成更难的合法任务时才采用它们。模型权重和验证标准保持固定。在数学、编程和科学领域，经过十四个演化轮次，求解器平均准确率从100.0%降至54.8%。消融实验表明，结合两种更新计划比固定框架递归或任一单独计划生成更难的任务。所得数据提升了下游SFT和GRPO性能。特别地，一个27B学生模型在1万条合成数学示例上微调后，在APEX上达到62.5%的mean-16准确率，与选定的前沿模型参考结果相比具有竞争力。这些结果支持在调整任务的同时调整合成框架，以生成日益具有挑战性且具有下游训练价值的数据。

Original abstract: Generating progressively harder reasoning problems requires synthesis procedures that adapt as the task distribution evolves. Existing task-level recursion reuses generated problems as seeds but leaves the construction harness unchanged. We present task-harness co-evolution, a framework for recursive harness self-improvement (RSI) in reasoning-data synthesis. Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation. Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch, adopting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria remain fixed. Across mathematics, coding, and science, mean solver accuracy decreases from 100.0% to 54.8% over fourteen evolution rounds. Ablations show that combining both update schedules produces harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone. The resulting data improves downstream SFT and GRPO performance. In particular, a 27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX, competitive with selected frontier-model references. These results support adapting the synthesis harness alongside the tasks to generate increasingly challenging data with downstream training value.

</details>


### [53] [Knowledge or Calculator? Decomposing the Skill Premium in Verifiable Financial Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03564)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 10 pages, 1 figure, 9 tables

Selection reason: 分解金融 agent 工作流中技能包、文档与可执行工具的贡献，对 agent 资源/harness 设计有实证迁移价值。

TL;DR: FinSkillBench评估显示，精选技能包显著提升金融AI智能体表现；可执行工具增益最大，文档次之，二者结合次可加；技能溢价依赖工作流与评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融AI智能体需执行正确量化、可靠使用程序性资源并产出可审计结构化结果，但现有评估难以覆盖这些要求。

Method: 提出FinSkillBench，包含2,603个时点情景、12个子任务、隐藏可再生基准真值和任务特定确定性验证器；跨9个模型和3种资源条件执行17,820个情景，对8个模型配对分析，并分别考察人工文档与可执行工具；用10种评分变体、聚类自助法和第二套独立评测框架验证。

Result: 精选技能包平均得分提高+16.2分（0.366至0.528），单情景生成技能仅+0.5分且更耗token和轮次；仅文档+5.6分，仅工具+19.5分，结合次可加；效应方向稳定，幅度受工具与数据暴露方式影响。

Conclusion: 技能溢价是完整模型、资源和评测框架系统的属性，而非仅由底层模型决定。

中文摘要：金融AI智能体必须做的不仅仅是检索事实：投资工作流要求正确的量化执行、对程序性资源的可靠使用以及可审计的结构化输出。我们提出FinSkillBench，一个评估套件，包含2,603个时点情景，涵盖投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析中的12个子任务，并配有隐藏且可再生的基准真值和任务特定的确定性验证器。通过跨9个模型和3种资源条件执行17,820个情景，对8个模型的配对分析显示，精选技能包将平均得分提高+16.2分（从0.366到0.528），而在单个情景内生成的技能仅增加+0.5分，同时消耗更多token和交互轮次。我们随后通过分别给予人工编写的程序性文档和可执行领域工具来分解精选技能带来的增益：仅文档增加+5.6分，仅工具增加+19.5分，而二者结合呈现次可加性。该增益强烈依赖于工作流：可执行工具在数值密集型工作流中占主导，当程序性或输出模式指导成为瓶颈时文档更为重要，而解释性任务则同时受益于两者。这些效应在10种评分变体和聚类自助法分析中符号稳定，且独立实现的第二套评测框架复现了方向性模式，同时表明效应幅度取决于工具和数据如何被暴露。总体而言，测得的“技能溢价”是整个模型、资源和评测框架系统的属性，而不仅仅是底层模型本身的属性。

Original abstract: Financial AI agents must do more than retrieve facts: investment workflows require correct quantitative execution, reliable use of procedural resources, and auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite of 2,603 point in time episodes across 12 subtasks in portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, with hidden regenerable ground truth and task specific deterministic verifiers. Executing 17,820 episodes across 9 models and 3 resource conditions, the paired analysis across 8 models shows that curated skill packages raise mean scores by +16.2 points (0.366 to 0.528), whereas skills generated within a single episode add only +0.5 points while consuming more tokens and turns. We then decompose the curated premium by granting human authored procedural documents and executable domain tools separately: documents alone add +5.6 points, tools alone add +19.5 points, and their combination is subadditive. The premium is strongly workflow dependent: executable tools dominate numerically intensive workflows, documentation matters more when procedural or output schema guidance is the bottleneck, and interpretive tasks benefit from both. The effects are sign stable across 10 scoring variants and cluster bootstrap analyses, and an independently implemented second harness reproduces the directional pattern while showing that effect magnitudes depend on how tools and data are exposed. Overall, a measured "skill premium" is a property of the full model, resource, and harness system rather than of the underlying model alone.

</details>


### [54] [HyperBrowseComp: A Multilingual and Multimodal Stress Test for Web-Browsing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03574)
*Alham Fikri Aji,Faiz Rizki Ramadhan,Zayd M. K. Zuhri,Seung Hun Eddie Han,Ryandito Diandaru,Qinrong Cui,Jan Christian Blaise Cruz,Badrinath Chandana,Peerawat Chomphooyod,Ahmed Attia,Jonibek Mansurov,Emilio Villa-Cueva,Canh Duong Nguyen,Imran Turganov,Minghao Wu,Peerat Limkonchotiwat,Irina Nikishina*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多语言多模态 web-browsing agent 长程检索 benchmark，可迁移至 agent 工具使用与评测。

TL;DR: HyperBrowseComp 是一个覆盖 13 种语言、423 道人工撰写验证问题的多语言多模态浏览基准，问题需通过开放网络中的多步线索与异构证据检索才能回答，并用于评估模型持续信息寻求能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个极具挑战性的测试平台，用于评估跨语言、跨证据模态的持续信息寻求，避免问题仅靠模型参数化知识即可回答。

Method: 人工撰写并验证 423 个问题，覆盖 13 种语言；每个问题要求可公开验证的简洁答案，依赖冷门证据、多步线索链或视频、扫描文档、图像、地图等异构来源；用无互联网模型过滤较简单问题；在统一智能体协议下，使用提供商原生搜索与共享外部检索套件评估若干模型，并对部分问题进行人工评估。

Result: 构建了 HyperBrowseComp 基准；评估了多个模型在统一协议下的表现，并结合人工评估样本来参照模型性能与投入；问题难度来自在开放网络上发现并连接证据。

Conclusion: HyperBrowseComp 为跨语言和证据模态的持续信息寻求提供了具有挑战性的测试平台，其难度核心在于开放网络中的证据发现与连接。

中文摘要：我们介绍了 HyperBrowseComp，一个多语言、多模态浏览基准，包含 423 个由母语者或高度熟练者撰写并经人工验证的问题，覆盖 13 种语言。问题被设计得极具挑战性。每个问题都指向一个简洁、可公开验证的答案，发现该答案需要定位冷门证据、遵循多步线索链，或检查异构来源，例如视频、扫描文档、图像或地图。通过使用无法访问互联网的模型对问题进行评估，过滤掉较简单的问题，以降低它们仅凭参数化知识即可被回答的可能性。我们在统一的智能体协议下，使用提供商原生搜索和共享外部检索套件评估了若干模型。为了给模型性能和投入提供参照，我们还对一部分问题进行了人工评估。HyperBrowseComp 为跨语言和证据模态的持续信息寻求提供了一个具有挑战性的测试平台，其难度源于在开放网络上发现并连接证据。

Original abstract: We introduce HyperBrowseComp, a multilingual and multimodal browsing benchmark comprising 423 manually authored and human-validated questions across 13 languages, written by native or highly proficient speakers. Questions are designed to be extremely challenging. Each question targets a concise, publicly verifiable answer whose discovery requires locating obscure evidence, following multi-step clue chains, or inspecting heterogeneous sources such as videos, scanned documents, images, or maps. Easier questions are filtered out by evaluating them with models without internet access to reduce the likelihood that they can be answered with parametric knowledge alone. We evaluate several models using provider-native search and a shared external retrieval harness under a common agent protocol. To contextualize model performance and effort, we also conduct a human evaluation on a sample of the questions. HyperBrowseComp provides a challenging testbed for persistent information seeking across languages and evidence modalities, with difficulty arising from discovering and connecting evidence on the open web.

</details>


### [55] [HazardWeaver: Scientific Route Selection for Hazard Analysis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03591)
*Wangshu Zhu,Xueqi Cheng,Liang Wu,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 24 pages, including references and appendices. Code is available at https://github.com/LabRAI/HazardWeaver

Selection reason: 提出状态依赖科学路线选择 agent，含知识编译、能力图与动态修订，可迁移至规划型 agent。

TL;DR: HazardWeaver将灾害分析中的科学路线选择形式化为状态依赖问题，结合证据条件与能力图来选择、执行并动态修正适用且可执行的路线；在包含141个实例的Hazard Weaver Benchmark上，其表现优于现有智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害理解与评估对防灾减灾至关重要，大语言模型智能体在灾害分析中受到关注，但自动化需根据事件、数据和工具判断方法适用性与可执行性，并随证据与执行结果变化重新选择。

Method: 提出状态依赖的科学路线选择问题与HazardWeaver框架：Hazard Knowledge Compiler提取证据关联的适用条件，Hazard Capability Graph表示可执行能力并检查输入输出兼容性，Hazard Weaver Agent据此选择、执行并修正路线。

Result: 构建Hazard Weaver Benchmark，含141个实例，覆盖7个单一灾害领域和4类多灾害交互；评估输出正确性、路线有效性和有正当理由的弃权。实验显示HazardWeaver优于现有智能体系统，在多条合格路线任务上提升最大。

Conclusion: HazardWeaver通过互补表示与动态修正有效支持灾害分析中的科学路线选择，代码已公开。
